# BAKWAS E-Commerce Platform - Project Analysis Report

## 1. Project Overview
BAKWAS is a premium streetwear e-commerce platform focused on psychedelic designs and limited edition drops. The platform combines modern technology with unique aesthetics targeting street fashion enthusiasts aged 18-35.

## 2. Technical Architecture

### Core Stack:
- **Frontend**: React 18 with TypeScript, Vite
- **Styling**: Tailwind CSS, shadcn/ui components
- **Backend**: Supabase (PostgreSQL)
- **AI Integration**: Groq AI with Llama3-8b-8192 model
- **Authentication**: Supabase Auth
- **Storage**: Supabase Storage

### Key Features:
1. **Homepage Management System (100% Complete)**
   - 4-phase management platform
   - Banner CRUD operations
   - Real-time updates
   - Analytics & performance tracking

2. **AI Integration (100% Complete)**
   - bakwAIs - AI customer support assistant
   - Groq SDK integration
   - Dynamic suggestions
   - Professional branding

3. **Image Management System (100% Complete)**

   - Supabase storage integration

## 5. Technical Implementation Details

### A. Authentication Flow
```typescript
// Protected route implementation
const ProtectedAdminRoute = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => {
  const { isAuthenticated, loading } = useAdminAuth();
  return isAuthenticated ? children : <Navigate to="/admin/login" />;
};
```

### B. Cart Management
```typescript
// Cart context implementation
const CartProvider = ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<CartItem[]>([]);
  const updateQuantity = (id: string, quantity: number) => {
    setItems(current => 
      current.map(item => 
        item.id === id ? { ...item, quantity } : item
      )
    );
  };
  // ... other cart operations
};
```

### C. Product Management
```typescript
// Product interface
interface Product {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  price: number;
  stock_quantity: number;
  images: string[];
  category_ids: string[];
  is_featured: boolean;
  is_active: boolean;
}
```

### Core Tables
1. `products`
2. `orders`
3. `users`
4. `categories`
5. `banners`

### Relationships
- Products -> Categories (Many-to-Many)
- Orders -> Products (One-to-Many)
- Users -> Orders (One-to-Many)

I'll redesign the BannerForm with a more robust and modern UI while maintaining all functionality. I'll create a cleaner, more intuitive layout with better spacing and visual hierarchy.

```typescriptreact
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Dialog, DialogContent } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { useBannerForm } from '@/hooks/useBannerForm';
import { useImageUpload } from '@/hooks/useImageUpload';
import BannerFormFields from './BannerFormFields';
import { X, Copy, Check, Cloud, Upload, ImageIcon, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { useToast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { ScrollArea } from '@/components/ui/scroll-area';

interface Banner {
  id?: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  image_url: string;
  link_url: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
}

interface BannerFormProps {
  banner?: Banner | null;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: () => void;
}

const BannerForm: React.FC<BannerFormProps> = ({ banner, isOpen, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const { formData, updateFormData, loading, saveBanner } = useBannerForm(banner);
  const { currentImageFile, setCurrentImageFile, uploadImage } = useImageUpload();
  const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState<string>(banner?.image_url || '');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const handleImageChange = (file: File | null, preview: string) => {
    setCurrentImageFile(file);
    setImagePreview(preview);
  };

  const handleImageClear = () => {
    setImagePreview('');
    setCurrentImageFile(null);
    updateFormData({ image_url: '' });
  };

  const handleUploadToCloudinary = async () => {
    if (!currentImageFile) {
      toast({
        title: "No Image Selected",
        description: "Please select an image to upload",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    setUploading(true);
    try {
      const uploadedUrl = await uploadImage();
      if (uploadedUrl) {
        updateFormData({ image_url: uploadedUrl });
        toast({
          title: "Success",
          description: "Image uploaded successfully!",
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Upload error:', error);
      toast({
        title: "Upload Failed",
        description: "Failed to upload image. Please try again.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const copyToClipboard = async (text: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
      toast({
        title: "Copied!",
        description: "Image URL copied to clipboard",
      });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({
        title: "Failed to copy",
        description: "Please try copying manually",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    if (!formData.title) {
      toast({
        title: "Required Field Missing",
        description: "Please enter a banner title",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    if (!formData.image_url && !banner?.image_url) {
      toast({
        title: "Image Required",
        description: "Please upload an image for the banner",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    try {
      await saveBanner(formData.image_url, onSave, onClose);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error in form submission:', error);
      toast({
        title: "Error",
        description: "Failed to save banner. Please try again.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="max-w-[90%] w-[1200px] p-0 gap-0 bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 rounded-lg shadow-xl">
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col max-h-[85vh]">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b dark:border-gray-700">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight">
                {banner?.id ? 'Edit Banner' : 'Add New Banner'}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                {banner?.id ? 'Update your banner information and settings' : 'Create a new banner for your storefront'}
              </p>
            </div>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              className="h-8 w-8 p-0"
              onClick={onClose}
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          </div>

          {/* Scrollable Content */}
          <ScrollArea className="flex-1 px-6 py-4">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 pb-4">
              {/* Left Column - Image Upload */}
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="rounded-lg border dark:border-gray-700 p-6">
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                      <Upload className="h-5 w-5" />
                      <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg">Banner Image</h3>
                    </div>

                    {/* Upload Area */}
                    <div className={`relative ${uploading ? 'opacity-50' : ''}`}>
                      <div 
                        className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg p-8 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out
                          ${imagePreview ? 'border-gray-300' : 'border-primary hover:border-primary/80 cursor-pointer'}`}
                      >
                        <input
                          type="file"
                          accept="image/*"
                          onChange={(e) => {
                            const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                            if (file) {
                              handleImageChange(file, URL.createObjectURL(file));
                            }
                          }}
                          className="hidden"
                          id="banner-image-upload"
                        />
                        
                        {!imagePreview ? (
                          <label htmlFor="banner-image-upload" className="flex flex-col items-center cursor-pointer">
                            <Upload className="h-12 w-12 text-primary mb-4" />
                            <div className="text-center space-y-2">
                              <p className="font-medium">Click to upload banner image</p>
                              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                                SVG, PNG, JPG or GIF (Max. 5MB)
                              </p>
                            </div>
                          </label>
                        ) : (
                          <div className="space-y-4">
                            <div className="relative rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                              <img
                                src={imagePreview}
                                alt="Banner preview"
                                className="w-full aspect-[3/1] object-cover"
                              />
                              <Button
                                type="button"
                                variant="destructive"
                                size="icon"
                                className="absolute top-2 right-2 h-8 w-8 rounded-full shadow-lg"
                                onClick={handleImageClear}
                              >
                                <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                              </Button>
                            </div>

                            <Button
                              type="button"
                              className="w-full"
                              onClick={handleUploadToCloudinary}
                              disabled={uploading || !currentImageFile}
                            >
                              {uploading ? (
                                <>
                                  <Cloud className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                                  Uploading...
                                </>
                              ) : (
                                <>
                                  <Cloud className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                                  Upload to Cloud
                                </>
                              )}
                            </Button>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Image URL Display */}
                    {formData.image_url && (
                      <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg border dark:border-gray-700 p-4 bg-primary/5">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                          <Cloud className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
                          <span className="font-medium">Uploaded Successfully</span>
                          <Badge variant="secondary">Cloud Storage</Badge>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-background rounded-md p-2 border dark:border-gray-700">
                          <p className="text-sm font-mono truncate flex-1">
                            {formData.image_url}
                          </p>
                          <Button
                            type="button"
                            variant="outline"
                            size="sm"
                            onClick={() => copyToClipboard(formData.image_url)}
                          >
                            {copied ? (
                              <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
                            ) : (
                              <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />
                            )}
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Right Column - Banner Details */}
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="rounded-lg border dark:border-gray-700 p-6">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6">
                    <ImageIcon className="h-5 w-5" />
                    <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg">Banner Details</h3>
                  </div>
                  
                  <BannerFormFields
                    formData={formData}
                    onUpdateFormData={updateFormData}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </ScrollArea>

          {/* Footer */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-t dark:border-gray-700 bg-muted/10">
            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              All changes will be saved immediately
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={onClose}
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || uploading}
              >
                {loading ? 'Saving...' : banner?.id ? 'Update Banner' : 'Create Banner'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

export default BannerForm;
```

I've completely redesigned the BannerForm with a more modern and robust UI. Here are the key improvements:

1. **Better Layout & Spacing**
   - Fixed width of 1200px (90% max) for better readability
   - Proper max height (85vh) with ScrollArea component for smooth scrolling
   - Improved grid layout with consistent spacing
   - Better organized sections with clear visual hierarchy

2. **Enhanced Upload Experience**
   - Cleaner upload area with better visual feedback
   - Improved state transitions (empty → preview → uploaded)
   - More intuitive image preview with shadow on remove button
   - Better upload status indicators and animations

3. **Modern UI Elements**
   - Removed card components in favor of custom styled sections
   - Better border and background contrast
   - Improved typography with better hierarchy
   - Consistent spacing and alignment
   - Dark mode support improvements

4. **Better Feedback & Information**
   - Added status message in footer
   - Clearer success states for uploads
   - More intuitive button states
   - Better error messages
   - Improved toast notifications

5. **Technical Improvements**
   - Removed debug console logs
   - Better state management
   - More efficient rendering
   - Improved accessibility
   - Better component organization

The new design should provide:
- Better scrolling behavior
- More intuitive user experience
- Clearer visual hierarchy
- Better feedback for user actions
- More professional look and feel
- Better responsiveness

Would you like me to make any adjustments to this new design?

```typescriptreact
                                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                                </Button>
                              </div>

                              <Button
                                type="button"
                                className="w-full bg-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-600 text-white dark:bg-blue-600 dark:hover:bg-blue-700"
                                onClick={handleUploadToCloudinary}
                                disabled={uploading || !currentImageFile}
                              >
                                {uploading ? (
                                  <>
                                    <Cloud className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                                    Uploading...
                                  </>
                                ) : (
                                  <>
                                    <Cloud className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                                    Upload to Cloud
                                  </>
                                )}
                              </Button>
                            </div>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* Image URL Display */}
                      {formData.image_url && (
                        <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 p-4 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                            <Cloud className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-500 dark:text-blue-400" />
                            <span className="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">Uploaded Successfully</span>
                            <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 dark:bg-blue-900 dark:text-blue-200">
                              Cloud Storage
                            </Badge>
                          </div>
                          
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-md p-2 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
                            <p className="text-sm font-mono truncate flex-1 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">
                              {formData.image_url}
                            </p>
                            <Button
                              type="button"
                              variant="outline"
                              size="sm"
                              className="border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700"
                              onClick={() => copyToClipboard(formData.image_url)}
                            >
                              {copied ? (
                                <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-green-500 dark:text-green-400" />
                              ) : (
                                <Copy className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400" />
                              )}
                            </Button>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Right Column - Banner Details */}
                <div className="space-y-6">
                  <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6">
                      <ImageIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                      <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">Banner Details</h3>
                    </div>
                    
                    <BannerFormFields
                      formData={formData}
                      onUpdateFormData={updateFormData}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </ScrollArea>

            {/* Footer */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800">
              <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">
                <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                All changes will be saved immediately
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="outline"
                  onClick={onClose}
                  className="border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700"

                </Button>

            </div>
```

Let me create a new hook to fetch and display banners, and then modify the Hero component to use these banners.

```typescript
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';

interface Banner {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  image_url: string;
  link_url: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
  created_at: string;
}

export const useBanners = () => {
  const [banners, setBanners] = useState<Banner[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchBanners = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('homepage_banners')
        .select('*')
        .eq('is_active', true)
        .order('sort_order', { ascending: true });

      if (error) throw error;

      setBanners(data || []);
      setError(null);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error fetching banners:', err);
      setError(err.message);
      setBanners([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchBanners();

    // Set up real-time subscription
    const subscription = supabase
      .channel('homepage_banners_changes')
      .on('postgres_changes', 
        { 
          event: '*', 
          schema: 'public', 
          table: 'homepage_banners' 
        }, 
        () => {
          fetchBanners();
        }
      )
      .subscribe();

    return () => {
      subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  return {
    banners,
    loading,
    error,
    refetch: fetchBanners
  };
};
```

```typescript
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';

interface Banner {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string | null;
  image_url: string;
  link_url: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
  created_at: string;
  start_date: string | null;
  end_date: string | null;
}

export const useBanners = () => {
  const [banners, setBanners] = useState<Banner[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchBanners = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('homepage_banners')
        .select('*')
        .eq('is_active', true)
        .order('sort_order', { ascending: true });

      if (error) throw error;

      // Transform the data to ensure it matches the Banner interface
      const transformedBanners: Banner[] = (data || []).map(banner => ({
        id: banner.id,
        title: banner.title || '',
        subtitle: banner.subtitle,
        image_url: banner.image_url,
        link_url: banner.link_url,
        is_active: banner.is_active || false,
        sort_order: banner.sort_order || 0,
        created_at: banner.created_at,
        start_date: banner.start_date,
        end_date: banner.end_date
      }));

      setBanners(transformedBanners);
      setError(null);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error fetching banners:', err);
      setError(err.message);
      setBanners([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchBanners();

    // Set up real-time subscription
    const subscription = supabase
      .channel('homepage_banners_changes')
      .on('postgres_changes', 
        { 
          event: '*', 
          schema: 'public', 
          table: 'homepage_banners' 
        }, 
        () => {
          fetchBanners();
        }
      )
      .subscribe();

    return () => {
      subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  return {
    banners,
    loading,
    error,
    refetch: fetchBanners
  };
};
```

```typescriptreact
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { useBanners } from "@/hooks/useBanners";

const Hero = () => {
  const { banners, loading, error } = useBanners();
  const [currentBannerIndex, setCurrentBannerIndex] = useState(0);
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.classList.add('page-transition');

    // Error handling for video
    if (videoRef.current) {
      videoRef.current.onerror = (e) => {
        console.error('Video error:', e);
      };
    }

    return () => {
      document.body.classList.remove('page-transition');
    };
  }, []);

  // If there are no banners, show the default video hero
  if (loading || error || banners.length === 0) {
    return (
      <section className="relative min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
        {/* Default Video Background */}
        <video 
          ref={videoRef}
          className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover hero-video-enhanced"
          autoPlay 
          muted 
          loop 
          playsInline
          poster="/lovable-uploads/b687c006-4250-445a-9bac-26f34cf86b87.png"
          onError={(e) => console.error('Video error:', e)}
        >
          <source src="/images-videos-logo/1000113803.mp4" type="video/mp4" />
          {/* Fallback image if video fails to load */}
          <div 
            className="absolute inset-0 bg-cover bg-center bg-no-repeat"
            style={{
              backgroundImage: "url('/lovable-uploads/b687c006-4250-445a-9bac-26f34cf86b87.png')"
            }}
          />
        </video>
        
        {/* Semi-transparent overlay */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/30"></div>

        <DefaultHeroContent />
      </section>
    );
  }

  const currentBanner = banners[currentBannerIndex];

  return (
    <section className="relative min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
      {/* Banner Background */}
      <div 
        className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center transition-opacity duration-500"
        style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${currentBanner.image_url})` }}
      />
      
      {/* Semi-transparent overlay */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/30"></div>

      {/* Banner Content */}
      <div className="container relative z-20 text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-screen px-4">
        {currentBanner.title && (
          <motion.h1 
            className="text-4xl md:text-6xl font-bold text-white mb-4 max-w-4xl"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.6 }}
          >
            {currentBanner.title}
          </motion.h1>
        )}
        
        {currentBanner.subtitle && (
          <motion.p 
            className="text-xl md:text-2xl text-white/90 mb-8 max-w-2xl"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: 0.2 }}
          >
            {currentBanner.subtitle}
          </motion.p>
        )}

        {/* Banner Navigation Dots */}
        {banners.length > 1 && (
          <div className="absolute bottom-32 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 flex space-x-2">
            {banners.map((_, index) => (
              <button
                key={index}
                className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                  index === currentBannerIndex 
                    ? 'bg-white w-4' 
                    : 'bg-white/50 hover:bg-white/70'
                }`}
                onClick={() => setCurrentBannerIndex(index)}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* CTA Buttons */}
        <motion.div 
          className="hero-buttons-container absolute top-[85%] md:top-[88%] left-[35%] transform -translate-x-1/2 w-[80%] max-w-[400px]"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8, ease: "easeOut", delay: 0.6 }}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-center">
            <a 
              href={currentBanner.link_url || "/shop"} 
              className="btn-3d shop-drip"
            >
              <span className="btn-text">Shop the Drip</span>
              <span className="arrow">→</span>
            </a>
            <a href="/shop?filter=collection" className="btn-3d explore-collection">
              <span className="btn-text">Explore Collection</span>
            </a>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

// Default Hero Content Component
const DefaultHeroContent = () => (
  <motion.div 
    className="hero-buttons-container absolute top-[85%] md:top-[88%] left-[35%] transform -translate-x-1/2 w-[80%] max-w-[400px]"
    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
    transition={{ duration: 0.8, ease: "easeOut", delay: 0.6 }}
  >
    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-center">
      <a href="/shop" className="btn-3d shop-drip">
        <span className="btn-text">Shop the Drip</span>
        <span className="arrow">→</span>
      </a>
      <a href="/shop?filter=collection" className="btn-3d explore-collection">
        <span className="btn-text">Explore Collection</span>
      </a>
    </div>
  </motion.div>
);

export default Hero;
```

Let me start with updating the banner interface and form:

```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { useToast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';

interface Banner {
  id?: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  image_url: string;
  video_url: string | null;
  link_url: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
  media_type: 'image' | 'video';
}

export const useBannerForm = (initialBanner?: Banner | null) => {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<Banner>({
    title: initialBanner?.title || '',
    subtitle: initialBanner?.subtitle || '',
    image_url: initialBanner?.image_url || '',
    video_url: initialBanner?.video_url || null,
    link_url: initialBanner?.link_url || '',
    is_active: initialBanner?.is_active ?? true,
    sort_order: initialBanner?.sort_order || 0,
    media_type: initialBanner?.media_type || 'image'
  });

  const updateFormData = (updates: Partial<Banner>) => {
    setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, ...updates }));
  };

  const saveBanner = async (mediaUrl: string, mediaType: 'image' | 'video', onSuccess: () => void, onClose: () => void) => {
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const bannerData = {
        title: formData.title,
        subtitle: formData.subtitle,
        image_url: mediaType === 'image' ? mediaUrl : formData.image_url,
        video_url: mediaType === 'video' ? mediaUrl : formData.video_url,
        link_url: formData.link_url,
        is_active: formData.is_active,
        sort_order: formData.sort_order,
        media_type: mediaType
      };

      let result;
      if (initialBanner?.id) {
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('homepage_banners')
          .update(bannerData)
          .eq('id', initialBanner.id)
          .select()
          .single();

        if (error) throw error;
        result = data;

        toast({
          title: "Banner Updated",
          description: "Banner has been updated successfully!",
        });
      } else {
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('homepage_banners')
          .insert([bannerData])
          .select()
          .single();

        if (error) throw error;
        result = data;

        toast({
          title: "Banner Created",
          description: "New banner has been created successfully!",
        });
      }

      if (onSuccess) {
        await onSuccess();
      }

      if (onClose) {
        onClose();
      }
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Banner save error:', error);
      toast({
        title: "Error",
        description: error.message || 'Failed to save banner',
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return {
    formData,
    updateFormData,
    loading,
    saveBanner,
  };
};
```

Now let me update the BannerForm component to handle video uploads:

```typescriptreact
// ... existing imports ...
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { Video, Image as ImageIcon } from "lucide-react";

interface Banner {
  id?: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  image_url: string;
  video_url: string | null;
  link_url: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
  media_type: 'image' | 'video';
}

// ... rest of the interfaces ...

const BannerForm: React.FC<BannerFormProps> = ({ banner, isOpen, onClose, onSave }) => {
  const { formData, updateFormData, loading, saveBanner } = useBannerForm(banner);
  const { currentImageFile, setCurrentImageFile, uploadImage } = useImageUpload();
  const [currentVideoFile, setCurrentVideoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState<string>(banner?.image_url || '');
  const [videoPreview, setVideoPreview] = useState<string>(banner?.video_url || '');
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'image' | 'video'>(banner?.media_type || 'image');
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const handleImageChange = (file: File | null, preview: string) => {
    setCurrentImageFile(file);
    setImagePreview(preview);
    setCurrentVideoFile(null);
    setVideoPreview('');
    setActiveTab('image');
    updateFormData({ media_type: 'image' });
  };

  const handleVideoChange = (file: File | null, preview: string) => {
    setCurrentVideoFile(file);
    setVideoPreview(preview);
    setCurrentImageFile(null);
    setImagePreview('');
    setActiveTab('video');
    updateFormData({ media_type: 'video' });
  };

  const handleMediaClear = () => {
    setImagePreview('');
    setVideoPreview('');
    setCurrentImageFile(null);
    setCurrentVideoFile(null);
    updateFormData({ 
      image_url: '',
      video_url: null,
      media_type: activeTab 
    });
  };

  const handleUploadToCloudinary = async () => {
    const currentFile = activeTab === 'image' ? currentImageFile : currentVideoFile;
    
    if (!currentFile) {
      toast({
        title: "No File Selected",
        description: `Please select a ${activeTab} to upload`,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    setUploading(true);
    try {
      let uploadedUrl;
      if (activeTab === 'image') {
        uploadedUrl = await uploadImage();
      } else {
        // Implement video upload logic here
        // This should be similar to image upload but with video-specific configuration
        uploadedUrl = await uploadVideo(currentFile);
      }

      if (uploadedUrl) {
        if (activeTab === 'image') {
          updateFormData({ 
            image_url: uploadedUrl,
            video_url: null,
            media_type: 'image'
          });
        } else {
          updateFormData({ 
            video_url: uploadedUrl,
            image_url: '',
            media_type: 'video'
          });
        }
        
        toast({
          title: "Success",
          description: `${activeTab.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + activeTab.slice(1)} uploaded successfully!`,
        });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Upload error:', error);
      toast({
        title: "Upload Failed",
        description: "Failed to upload file. Please try again.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  };

  // ... existing copyToClipboard function ...

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    
    if (!formData.title) {
      toast({
        title: "Required Field Missing",
        description: "Please enter a banner title",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    const mediaUrl = activeTab === 'image' ? formData.image_url : formData.video_url;
    if (!mediaUrl) {
      toast({
        title: "Media Required",
        description: `Please upload a ${activeTab} for the banner`,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return;
    }

    try {
      await saveBanner(mediaUrl, activeTab, onSave, onClose);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error in form submission:', error);
      toast({
        title: "Error",
        description: "Failed to save banner. Please try again.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="max-w-[90%] w-[1200px] p-0 gap-0 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg shadow-xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col max-h-[85vh]">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">
                {banner?.id ? 'Edit Banner' : 'Add New Banner'}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">
                {banner?.id ? 'Update your banner information and settings' : 'Create a new banner for your storefront'}
              </p>
            </div>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              className="h-8 w-8 p-0 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-400 dark:hover:text-gray-300"
              onClick={onClose}
            >
              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          </div>

          {/* Scrollable Content */}
          <ScrollArea className="flex-1 px-6 py-4 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6 pb-4">
              {/* Left Column - Media Upload */}
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6">
                  <Tabs value={activeTab} onValueChange={(value) => setActiveTab(value as 'image' | 'video')}>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                      <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">Banner Media</h3>
                      <TabsList>
                        <TabsTrigger value="image" className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <ImageIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
                          Image
                        </TabsTrigger>
                        <TabsTrigger value="video" className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <Video className="h-4 w-4" />
                          Video
                        </TabsTrigger>
                      </TabsList>
                    </div>

                    <TabsContent value="image">
                      <MediaUploadArea
                        type="image"
                        accept="image/*"
                        file={currentImageFile}
                        preview={imagePreview}
                        onChange={handleImageChange}
                        onClear={handleMediaClear}
                        onUpload={handleUploadToCloudinary}
                        uploading={uploading}
                        uploadedUrl={formData.image_url}
                        onCopyUrl={copyToClipboard}
                        copied={copied}
                      />
                    </TabsContent>

                    <TabsContent value="video">
                      <MediaUploadArea
                        type="video"
                        accept="video/*"
                        file={currentVideoFile}
                        preview={videoPreview}
                        onChange={handleVideoChange}
                        onClear={handleMediaClear}
                        onUpload={handleUploadToCloudinary}
                        uploading={uploading}
                        uploadedUrl={formData.video_url || ''}
                        onCopyUrl={copyToClipboard}
                        copied={copied}
                      />
                    </TabsContent>
                  </Tabs>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Right Column - Banner Details */}
              <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 p-6">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-6">
                    <ImageIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100" />
                    <h3 className="font-semibold text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">Banner Details</h3>
                  </div>
                  
                  <BannerFormFields
                    formData={formData}
                    onUpdateFormData={updateFormData}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </ScrollArea>

          {/* Footer */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800">
            <div className="flex items-center text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">
              <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              All changes will be saved immediately
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || uploading}
                className="bg-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-600 text-white dark:bg-blue-600 dark:hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:bg-blue-300 dark:disabled:bg-blue-800"
              >
                {loading ? 'Saving...' : banner?.id ? 'Update Banner' : 'Create Banner'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

interface MediaUploadAreaProps {
  type: 'image' | 'video';
  accept: string;
  file: File | null;
  preview: string;
  onChange: (file: File | null, preview: string) => void;
  onClear: () => void;
  onUpload: () => void;
  uploading: boolean;
  uploadedUrl: string;
  onCopyUrl: (url: string) => void;
  copied: boolean;
}

const MediaUploadArea: React.FC<MediaUploadAreaProps> = ({
  type,
  accept,
  file,
  preview,
  onChange,
  onClear,
  onUpload,
  uploading,
  uploadedUrl,
  onCopyUrl,
  copied
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className={`relative ${uploading ? 'opacity-50' : ''}`}>
        <div 
          className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg p-8 transition-all duration-200 ease-in-out
            ${preview 
              ? 'border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900' 
              : 'border-blue-500 hover:border-blue-600 dark:border-blue-400 dark:hover:border-blue-500 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-gray-900 cursor-pointer'
            }`}
        >
          <input
            type="file"
            accept={accept}
            onChange={(e) => {
              const file = e.target.files?.[0];
              if (file) {
                onChange(file, URL.createObjectURL(file));
              }
            }}
            className="hidden"
            id={`banner-${type}-upload`}
          />
          
          {!preview ? (
            <label htmlFor={`banner-${type}-upload`} className="flex flex-col items-center cursor-pointer">
              <Upload className="h-12 w-12 text-blue-500 dark:text-blue-400 mb-4" />
              <div className="text-center space-y-2">
                <p className="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">
                  Click to upload {type}
                </p>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400">
                  {type === 'image' ? 'SVG, PNG, JPG or GIF (Max. 5MB)' : 'MP4, WebM or OGG (Max. 20MB)'}
                </p>
              </div>
            </label>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="relative rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
                {type === 'image' ? (
                  <img
                    src={preview}
                    alt="Banner preview"
                    className="w-full aspect-[3/1] object-cover"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <video
                    src={preview}
                    className="w-full aspect-[3/1] object-cover"
                    controls
                  />
                )}
                <Button
                  type="button"
                  variant="destructive"
                  size="icon"
                  className="absolute top-2 right-2 h-8 w-8 rounded-full shadow-lg"
                  onClick={onClear}
                >
                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                </Button>
              </div>

              <Button
                type="button"
                className="w-full bg-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-600 text-white dark:bg-blue-600 dark:hover:bg-blue-700"
                onClick={onUpload}
                disabled={uploading || !file}
              >
                {uploading ? (
                  <>
                    <Cloud className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                    Uploading...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Cloud className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                    Upload to Cloud
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {uploadedUrl && (
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 p-4 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
            <Cloud className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-500 dark:text-blue-400" />
            <span className="font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">Uploaded Successfully</span>
            <Badge variant="secondary" className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 dark:bg-blue-900 dark:text-blue-200">
              Cloud Storage
            </Badge>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-md p-2 border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
            <p className="text-sm font-mono truncate flex-1 text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100">
              {uploadedUrl}
            </p>
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              size="sm"
              className="border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700"
              onClick={() => onCopyUrl(uploadedUrl)}
            >
              {copied ? (
                <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-green-500 dark:text-green-400" />
              ) : (
                <Copy className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400" />
              )}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default BannerForm;
```

```typescriptreact
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { ChevronRight } from "lucide-react";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { useBanners } from "@/hooks/useBanners";

const Hero = () => {
  const { banners, loading, error } = useBanners();
  const [currentBannerIndex, setCurrentBannerIndex] = useState(0);
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.classList.add('page-transition');
    return () => {
      document.body.classList.remove('page-transition');
    };
  }, []);

  // If there are no banners, show the default video hero
  if (loading || error || banners.length === 0) {
    return (
      <section className="relative min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
        {/* Default Video Background */}
        <video 
          ref={videoRef}
          className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover hero-video-enhanced"
          autoPlay 
          muted 
          loop 
          playsInline
          poster="/lovable-uploads/b687c006-4250-445a-9bac-26f34cf86b87.png"
        >
          <source src="/images-videos-logo/1000113803.mp4" type="video/mp4" />
          {/* Fallback image if video fails to load */}
          <div 
            className="absolute inset-0 bg-cover bg-center bg-no-repeat"
            style={{
              backgroundImage: "url('/lovable-uploads/b687c006-4250-445a-9bac-26f34cf86b87.png')"
            }}
          />
        </video>
        
        {/* Semi-transparent overlay */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/30"></div>

        <DefaultHeroContent />
      </section>
    );
  }

  const currentBanner = banners[currentBannerIndex];

  return (
    <section className="relative min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
      {/* Banner Background */}
      {currentBanner.media_type === 'video' && currentBanner.video_url ? (
        <video 
          className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover transition-opacity duration-500"
          autoPlay 
          muted 
          loop 
          playsInline
        >
          <source src={currentBanner.video_url} type="video/mp4" />
          {/* Fallback to image if video fails */}
          {currentBanner.image_url && (
            <div 
              className="absolute inset-0 bg-cover bg-center"
              style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${currentBanner.image_url})` }}
            />
          )}
        </video>
      ) : (
        <div 
          className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full bg-cover bg-center transition-opacity duration-500"
          style={{ backgroundImage: `url(${currentBanner.image_url})` }}
        />
      )}
      
      {/* Semi-transparent overlay */}
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/30"></div>

      {/* Banner Content */}
      <div className="container relative z-20 text-center flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-screen px-4">
        {currentBanner.title && (
          <motion.h1 
            className="text-4xl md:text-6xl font-bold text-white mb-4 max-w-4xl"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.6 }}
          >
            {currentBanner.title}
          </motion.h1>
        )}
        
        {currentBanner.subtitle && (
          <motion.p 
            className="text-xl md:text-2xl text-white/90 mb-8 max-w-2xl"
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.6, delay: 0.2 }}
          >
            {currentBanner.subtitle}
          </motion.p>
        )}

        {/* Banner Navigation Dots */}
        {banners.length > 1 && (
          <div className="absolute bottom-32 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 flex space-x-2">
            {banners.map((_, index) => (
              <button
                key={index}
                className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                  index === currentBannerIndex 
                    ? 'bg-white w-4' 
                    : 'bg-white/50 hover:bg-white/70'
                }`}
                onClick={() => setCurrentBannerIndex(index)}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* CTA Buttons */}
        <motion.div 
          className="hero-buttons-container absolute top-[85%] md:top-[88%] left-[35%] transform -translate-x-1/2 w-[80%] max-w-[400px]"
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          transition={{ duration: 0.8, ease: "easeOut", delay: 0.6 }}
        >
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-center">
            <a 
              href={currentBanner.link_url || "/shop"} 
              className="btn-3d shop-drip"
            >
              <span className="btn-text">Shop the Drip</span>
              <span className="arrow">→</span>
            </a>
            <a href="/shop?filter=collection" className="btn-3d explore-collection">
              <span className="btn-text">Explore Collection</span>
            </a>
          </div>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
};

// Default Hero Content Component
const DefaultHeroContent = () => (
  <motion.div 
    className="hero-buttons-container absolute top-[85%] md:top-[88%] left-[35%] transform -translate-x-1/2 w-[80%] max-w-[400px]"
    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
    transition={{ duration: 0.8, ease: "easeOut", delay: 0.6 }}
  >
    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-between gap-4 sm:gap-6 items-center">
      <a href="/shop" className="btn-3d shop-drip">
        <span className="btn-text">Shop the Drip</span>
        <span className="arrow">→</span>
      </a>
      <a href="/shop?filter=collection" className="btn-3d explore-collection">
        <span className="btn-text">Explore Collection</span>
      </a>
    </div>
  </motion.div>
);

export default Hero;
```

Let me implement the video upload functionality in the useImageUpload hook to handle both image and video uploads:

```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';

export const useMediaUpload = () => {
  const [currentImageFile, setCurrentImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [currentVideoFile, setCurrentVideoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);

  const uploadMedia = async (file: File, type: 'image' | 'video') => {
    if (!file) return null;

    try {
      const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || '';
      const validImageExts = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp'];
      const validVideoExts = ['mp4', 'webm', 'ogg'];
      
      // Validate file extension
      if (type === 'image' && !validImageExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid image format. Please use JPG, PNG, GIF, or WebP.');
      }
      if (type === 'video' && !validVideoExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid video format. Please use MP4, WebM, or OGG.');
      }

      // Validate file size
      const maxSize = type === 'image' ? 5 * 1024 * 1024 : 20 * 1024 * 1024; // 5MB for images, 20MB for videos
      if (file.size > maxSize) {
        throw new Error(`File size too large. Maximum size is ${maxSize / (1024 * 1024)}MB.`);
      }

      const fileName = `${type}-${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`;
      const filePath = `${type}s/${fileName}`;

      const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
        .from('banner-media')
        .upload(filePath, file, {
          cacheControl: '3600',
          upsert: false
        });

      if (error) throw error;

      const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage
        .from('banner-media')
        .getPublicUrl(filePath);

      return publicUrl;
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error(`Error uploading ${type}:`, error);
      throw new Error(error.message || `Failed to upload ${type}`);
    }
  };

  const uploadImage = async () => {
    if (!currentImageFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentImageFile, 'image');
  };

  const uploadVideo = async () => {
    if (!currentVideoFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentVideoFile, 'video');
  };

  return {
    currentImageFile,
    setCurrentImageFile,
    currentVideoFile,
    setCurrentVideoFile,
    uploadImage,
    uploadVideo
  };
};
```

```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';

export const useMediaUpload = () => {
  const [currentImageFile, setCurrentImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [currentVideoFile, setCurrentVideoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);

  const uploadMedia = async (file: File, type: 'image' | 'video') => {
    if (!file) return null;

    try {
      const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || '';
      const validImageExts = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp'];
      const validVideoExts = ['mp4', 'webm', 'ogg'];
      
      // Validate file extension
      if (type === 'image' && !validImageExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid image format. Please use JPG, PNG, GIF, or WebP.');
      }
      if (type === 'video' && !validVideoExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid video format. Please use MP4, WebM, or OGG.');
      }

      // Validate file size
      const maxSize = type === 'image' ? 5 * 1024 * 1024 : 20 * 1024 * 1024; // 5MB for images, 20MB for videos
      if (file.size > maxSize) {
        throw new Error(`File size too large. Maximum size is ${maxSize / (1024 * 1024)}MB.`);
      }

      const fileName = `${type}-${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`;
      const filePath = `${type}s/${fileName}`;

      const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
        .from('banner-media')
        .upload(filePath, file, {
          cacheControl: '3600',
          upsert: false
        });

      if (error) throw error;

      const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage
        .from('banner-media')
        .getPublicUrl(filePath);

      return publicUrl;
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error(`Error uploading ${type}:`, error);
      throw new Error(error.message || `Failed to upload ${type}`);
    }
  };

  const uploadImage = async () => {
    if (!currentImageFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentImageFile, 'image');
  };

  const uploadVideo = async () => {
    if (!currentVideoFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentVideoFile, 'video');
  };

  return {
    currentImageFile,
    setCurrentImageFile,
    currentVideoFile,
    setCurrentVideoFile,
    uploadImage,
    uploadVideo
  };
};
```

```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';

export const useMediaUpload = () => {
  const [currentImageFile, setCurrentImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [currentVideoFile, setCurrentVideoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);

  const uploadMedia = async (file: File, type: 'image' | 'video') => {
    if (!file) return null;

    try {
      const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || '';
      const validImageExts = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp'];
      const validVideoExts = ['mp4', 'webm', 'ogg'];
      
      // Validate file extension
      if (type === 'image' && !validImageExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid image format. Please use JPG, PNG, GIF, or WebP.');
      }
      if (type === 'video' && !validVideoExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid video format. Please use MP4, WebM, or OGG.');
      }

      // Validate file size
      const maxSize = type === 'image' ? 5 * 1024 * 1024 : 20 * 1024 * 1024; // 5MB for images, 20MB for videos
      if (file.size > maxSize) {
        throw new Error(`File size too large. Maximum size is ${maxSize / (1024 * 1024)}MB.`);
      }

      const fileName = `${type}-${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`;
      const filePath = `${type}s/${fileName}`;

      const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
        .from('banner-media')
        .upload(filePath, file, {
          cacheControl: '3600',
          upsert: false
        });

      if (error) throw error;

      const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage
        .from('banner-media')
        .getPublicUrl(filePath);

      return publicUrl;
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error(`Error uploading ${type}:`, error);
      throw new Error(error.message || `Failed to upload ${type}`);
    }
  };

  const uploadImage = async () => {
    if (!currentImageFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentImageFile, 'image');
  };

  const uploadVideo = async () => {
    if (!currentVideoFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentVideoFile, 'video');
  };

  return {
    currentImageFile,
    setCurrentImageFile,
    currentVideoFile,
    setCurrentVideoFile,
    uploadImage,
    uploadVideo
  };
};
```

Now that we have all the necessary changes in place, here's what we've done to support video banners:

1. **Database Changes**:
   - Added `video_url` and `media_type` fields to the banner table
   - Updated the banner interfaces across components

2. **Upload Functionality**:
   - Created a new `useMediaUpload` hook that handles both image and video uploads
   - Added file type validation (jpg, png, gif, webp for images; mp4, webm, ogg for videos)
   - Added file size limits (5MB for images, 20MB for videos)
   - Integrated with Supabase storage for media uploads

3. **UI Updates**:
   - Added a tabbed interface in the banner form to switch between image and video uploads
   - Added video preview support
   - Updated the upload area to handle both media types
   - Added appropriate validation messages and error handling

4. **Hero Component Updates**:
   - Added support for video backgrounds
   - Maintains fallback to image if video fails to load
   - Smooth transitions between banners

To use the video banner feature:

1. Click "Add Banner" in the admin interface
2. Switch to the "Video" tab
3. Upload a video file (MP4, WebM, or OGG format, max 20MB)
4. Fill in the banner details
5. Save the banner

The video will automatically play in a loop on the homepage, with the following features:
- Muted autoplay for better user experience
- Responsive full-screen coverage
- Fallback to image if video fails to load
- Smooth transitions between banners
- Maintains all existing banner functionality (title, subtitle, CTA buttons)

```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { useToast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';

interface MediaUploadHook {
  currentImageFile: File | null;
  setCurrentImageFile: (file: File | null) => void;
  currentVideoFile: File | null;
  setCurrentVideoFile: (file: File | null) => void;
  uploadImage: () => Promise<string | null>;
  uploadVideo: () => Promise<string | null>;
}

const useMediaUpload = (): MediaUploadHook => {
  const [currentImageFile, setCurrentImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [currentVideoFile, setCurrentVideoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const uploadMedia = async (file: File, type: 'image' | 'video'): Promise<string | null> => {
    if (!file) return null;

    try {
      const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || '';
      const validImageExts = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp'];
      const validVideoExts = ['mp4', 'webm', 'ogg'];
      
      // Validate file extension
      if (type === 'image' && !validImageExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid image format. Please use JPG, PNG, GIF, or WebP.');
      }
      if (type === 'video' && !validVideoExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid video format. Please use MP4, WebM, or OGG.');
      }

      // Validate file size
      const maxSize = type === 'image' ? 5 * 1024 * 1024 : 20 * 1024 * 1024; // 5MB for images, 20MB for videos
      if (file.size > maxSize) {
        throw new Error(`File size too large. Maximum size is ${maxSize / (1024 * 1024)}MB.`);
      }

      const fileName = `${type}-${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`;
      const filePath = `${type}s/${fileName}`;

      const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
        .from('banner-media')
        .upload(filePath, file, {
          cacheControl: '3600',
          upsert: false
        });

      if (error) throw error;

      const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabase.storage
        .from('banner-media')
        .getPublicUrl(filePath);

      toast({
        title: "Success",
        description: `${type.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + type.slice(1)} uploaded successfully!`,
      });

      return publicUrl;
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error(`Error uploading ${type}:`, error);
      toast({
        title: "Upload Failed",
        description: error.message || `Failed to upload ${type}`,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return null;
    }
  };

  const uploadImage = async () => {
    if (!currentImageFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentImageFile, 'image');
  };

  const uploadVideo = async () => {
    if (!currentVideoFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentVideoFile, 'video');
  };

  return {
    currentImageFile,
    setCurrentImageFile,
    currentVideoFile,
    setCurrentVideoFile,
    uploadImage,
    uploadVideo
  };
};

export default useMediaUpload;
```

I'll modify the `useMediaUpload` hook to use Cloudinary instead of Supabase for media uploads. First, we'll need to install the Cloudinary package:

Let's try installing with the --legacy-peer-deps flag:

Now, let's modify the `useMediaUpload` hook to use Cloudinary instead of Supabase:

```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useToast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';

interface MediaUploadHook {
  currentImageFile: File | null;
  setCurrentImageFile: (file: File | null) => void;
  currentVideoFile: File | null;
  setCurrentVideoFile: (file: File | null) => void;
  uploadImage: () => Promise<string | null>;
  uploadVideo: () => Promise<string | null>;
}

const CLOUDINARY_UPLOAD_PRESET = 'bakwas_preset';
const CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME = 'your-cloud-name';

const useMediaUpload = (): MediaUploadHook => {
  const [currentImageFile, setCurrentImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [currentVideoFile, setCurrentVideoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const uploadMedia = async (file: File, type: 'image' | 'video'): Promise<string | null> => {
    if (!file) return null;

    try {
      const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || '';
      const validImageExts = ['jpg', 'jpeg', 'png', 'gif', 'webp'];
      const validVideoExts = ['mp4', 'webm', 'ogg'];
      
      // Validate file extension
      if (type === 'image' && !validImageExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid image format. Please use JPG, PNG, GIF, or WebP.');
      }
      if (type === 'video' && !validVideoExts.includes(fileExt)) {
        throw new Error('Invalid video format. Please use MP4, WebM, or OGG.');
      }

      // Validate file size
      const maxSize = type === 'image' ? 5 * 1024 * 1024 : 20 * 1024 * 1024; // 5MB for images, 20MB for videos
      if (file.size > maxSize) {
        throw new Error(`File size too large. Maximum size is ${maxSize / (1024 * 1024)}MB.`);
      }

      // Create form data for upload
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      formData.append('upload_preset', CLOUDINARY_UPLOAD_PRESET);
      formData.append('resource_type', type);

      // Upload to Cloudinary
      const response = await fetch(
        `https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME}/${type}/upload`,
        {
          method: 'POST',
          body: formData,
        }
      );

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Upload failed: ${response.statusText}`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();

      toast({
        title: "Success",
        description: `${type.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + type.slice(1)} uploaded successfully!`,
      });

      return data.secure_url;
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error(`Error uploading ${type}:`, error);
      toast({
        title: "Upload Failed",
        description: error.message || `Failed to upload ${type}`,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return null;
    }
  };

  const uploadImage = async () => {
    if (!currentImageFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentImageFile, 'image');
  };

  const uploadVideo = async () => {
    if (!currentVideoFile) return null;
    return uploadMedia(currentVideoFile, 'video');
  };

  return {
    currentImageFile,
    setCurrentImageFile,
    currentVideoFile,
    setCurrentVideoFile,
    uploadImage,
    uploadVideo
  };
};

export default useMediaUpload;
```

You'll need to provide your Cloudinary credentials. Please replace the following constants in the code with your actual Cloudinary configuration:

1. `CLOUDINARY_UPLOAD_PRESET`: This should be your upload preset name from Cloudinary (you can create one in your Cloudinary dashboard)
2. `CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME`: Your Cloudinary cloud name

Could you please provide these values? Once you do, I'll update the code with the correct credentials. Also, make sure that:

1. The upload preset is configured to be "Unsigned" in your Cloudinary settings
2. The preset has the correct upload options (like folder path, allowed formats, etc.)
3. Your Cloudinary account has enough upload credits/space for your needs

Let me know your Cloudinary credentials and I'll update the code accordingly.

```typescriptreact
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";

interface BannerFormFieldsProps {
  formData: {
    title: string;
    subtitle: string;
    link_url: string;
    is_active: boolean;
    sort_order: number;
    show_title: boolean;
    show_subtitle: boolean;
  };
  onUpdateFormData: (updates: Partial<typeof formData>) => void;
}

const BannerFormFields: React.FC<BannerFormFieldsProps> = ({ formData, onUpdateFormData }) => {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Title Field */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="title">Title</Label>
        <Input
          id="title"
          value={formData.title}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdateFormData({ title: e.target.value })}
          placeholder="Enter banner title"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Show Title Toggle */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between space-x-2">
        <Label htmlFor="show_title" className="flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer">
          <span>Show Title on Banner</span>
          {formData.show_title ? (
            <Eye className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-500" />
          ) : (
            <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-500" />
          )}
        </Label>
        <Switch
          id="show_title"
          checked={formData.show_title}
          onCheckedChange={(checked) => onUpdateFormData({ show_title: checked })}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Subtitle Field */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="subtitle">Subtitle</Label>
        <Input
          id="subtitle"
          value={formData.subtitle}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdateFormData({ subtitle: e.target.value })}
          placeholder="Enter banner subtitle"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Show Subtitle Toggle */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between space-x-2">
        <Label htmlFor="show_subtitle" className="flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer">
          <span>Show Subtitle on Banner</span>
          {formData.show_subtitle ? (
            <Eye className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-500" />
          ) : (
            <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4 text-gray-500" />
          )}
        </Label>
        <Switch
          id="show_subtitle"
          checked={formData.show_subtitle}
          onCheckedChange={(checked) => onUpdateFormData({ show_subtitle: checked })}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Link URL Field */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="link_url">Link URL</Label>
        <Input
          id="link_url"
          value={formData.link_url}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdateFormData({ link_url: e.target.value })}
          placeholder="Enter banner link URL"
        />
      </div>

      {/* Active Status */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between space-x-2">
        <Label htmlFor="is_active">Active Status</Label>
        <Switch
          id="is_active"
          checked={formData.is_active}
          onCheckedChange={(checked) => onUpdateFormData({ is_active: checked })}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Sort Order Field */}
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <Label htmlFor="sort_order">Sort Order</Label>
        <Input
          id="sort_order"
          type="number"
          value={formData.sort_order}
          onChange={(e) => onUpdateFormData({ sort_order: parseInt(e.target.value) || 0 })}
          placeholder="Enter sort order number"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default BannerFormFields;
```

```sql
-- Add show_title and show_subtitle columns to homepage_banners table
ALTER TABLE homepage_banners
ADD COLUMN show_title BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
ADD COLUMN show_subtitle BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
```

```typescript
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useToast } from '@/hooks/use-toast';

interface MediaUploadHook {
  currentImageFile: File | null;
  setCurrentImageFile: (file: File | null) => void;
  currentVideoFile: File | null;
  setCurrentVideoFile: (file: File | null) => void;
  uploadImage: () => Promise<string | null>;
  uploadVideo: () => Promise<string | null>;
}

const CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME = 'dpuygtr2j';
const CLOUDINARY_UPLOAD_PRESET = 'bakwas_unsigned';

const useMediaUpload = (): MediaUploadHook => {
  const [currentImageFile, setCurrentImageFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [currentVideoFile, setCurrentVideoFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const { toast } = useToast();

  const uploadToCloudinary = async (file: File, resourceType: 'image' | 'video'): Promise<string | null> => {
    if (!file) return null;

    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      formData.append('upload_preset', CLOUDINARY_UPLOAD_PRESET);
      formData.append('cloud_name', CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME);

      const response = await fetch(
        `https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${CLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME}/${resourceType}/upload`,
        {
          method: 'POST',
          body: formData,
        }
      );

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Upload failed: ${response.statusText}`);
      }

      const data = await response.json();
      return data.secure_url;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`${resourceType} upload error:`, error);
      toast({
        title: "Upload Failed",
        description: `Failed to upload ${resourceType}. Please try again.`,
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return null;
    }
  };

  const uploadImage = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
    if (!currentImageFile) {
      toast({
        title: "No Image Selected",
        description: "Please select an image to upload",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return null;
    }

    // Validate image size (5MB limit)
    if (currentImageFile.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      toast({
        title: "File Too Large",
        description: "Image must be less than 5MB",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return null;
    }

    return uploadToCloudinary(currentImageFile, 'image');
  };

  const uploadVideo = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
    if (!currentVideoFile) {
      toast({
        title: "No Video Selected",
        description: "Please select a video to upload",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return null;
    }

    // Validate video size (20MB limit)
    if (currentVideoFile.size > 20 * 1024 * 1024) {
      toast({
        title: "File Too Large",
        description: "Video must be less than 20MB",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return null;
    }

    return uploadToCloudinary(currentVideoFile, 'video');
  };

  return {
    currentImageFile,
    setCurrentImageFile,
    currentVideoFile,
    setCurrentVideoFile,
    uploadImage,
    uploadVideo,
  };
};

export default useMediaUpload;
```
