```typescript
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export const executeMigration = async () => {
  try {
    // Create blog_posts table
    await supabase.rpc('create_blog_tables', {
      sql: `
        -- Create blog_posts table with enhanced fields
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts (
            id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
            title TEXT NOT NULL,
            slug TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            content TEXT NOT NULL,
            excerpt TEXT,
            featured_image TEXT,
            author_name TEXT,
            categories TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}',
            tags TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}',
            is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
            publish_date TIMESTAMPTZ,
            created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
            updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
            status TEXT CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'review', 'scheduled', 'published', 'archived')) DEFAULT 'draft',
            post_type TEXT CHECK (post_type IN ('article', 'news', 'case_study', 'tutorial', 'opinion')) DEFAULT 'article',
            view_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            reading_time INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            seo_title TEXT,
            seo_description TEXT
        );

        -- Add indexes for better performance
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_slug_idx ON blog_posts(slug);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_status_idx ON blog_posts(status);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_publish_date_idx ON blog_posts(publish_date);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_created_at_idx ON blog_posts(created_at);

        -- Enable RLS
        ALTER TABLE blog_posts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

        -- Public read access for published posts
        CREATE POLICY "Public can view published posts" ON blog_posts
            FOR SELECT
            USING (is_active = true AND status = 'published');

        -- Admin full access
        CREATE POLICY "Admins have full access to posts" ON blog_posts
            FOR ALL
            USING (
                EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM admin_users
                    WHERE admin_users.email = auth.jwt() ->> 'email'
                )
            );
      `
    });

    toast.success('Blog system tables created successfully');
    return true;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Migration error:', error);
    toast.error('Failed to create blog system tables: ' + error.message);
    return false;
  }
};
```

I see the issue. Let's modify the approach to use the raw SQL query feature instead:

```typescript
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export const executeMigration = async () => {
  try {
    // Create blog_posts table
    const { error: tableError } = await supabase.from('blog_posts').select('id').limit(1);
    
    if (tableError?.code === 'PGRST204') {
      // Table doesn't exist, create it
      const { error: createError } = await supabase.sql`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts (
            id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
            title TEXT NOT NULL,
            slug TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            content TEXT NOT NULL,
            excerpt TEXT,
            featured_image TEXT,
            author_name TEXT,
            categories TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}',
            tags TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}',
            is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
            publish_date TIMESTAMPTZ,
            created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
            updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
            status TEXT CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'review', 'scheduled', 'published', 'archived')) DEFAULT 'draft',
            post_type TEXT CHECK (post_type IN ('article', 'news', 'case_study', 'tutorial', 'opinion')) DEFAULT 'article',
            view_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            reading_time INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            seo_title TEXT,
            seo_description TEXT
        );

        -- Add indexes for better performance
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_slug_idx ON blog_posts(slug);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_status_idx ON blog_posts(status);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_publish_date_idx ON blog_posts(publish_date);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_created_at_idx ON blog_posts(created_at);

        -- Enable RLS
        ALTER TABLE blog_posts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

        -- Public read access for published posts
        CREATE POLICY "Public can view published posts" ON blog_posts
            FOR SELECT
            USING (is_active = true AND status = 'published');

        -- Admin full access
        CREATE POLICY "Admins have full access to posts" ON blog_posts
            FOR ALL
            USING (
                EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM admin_users
                    WHERE admin_users.email = auth.jwt() ->> 'email'
                )
            );
      `;

      if (createError) {
        throw createError;
      }

      toast.success('Blog system tables created successfully');
    }

    return true;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Migration error:', error);
    toast.error('Failed to create blog system tables: ' + error.message);
    return false;
  }
};
```

```sql
    post_type TEXT CHECK (post_type IN ('article', 'news', 'case_study', 'tutorial', 'opinion')) DEFAULT 'article',
    view_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    reading_time INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    seo_title TEXT,
    seo_description TEXT
);

-- Add indexes for better performance
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_slug_idx ON blog_posts(slug);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_status_idx ON blog_posts(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_publish_date_idx ON blog_posts(publish_date);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_created_at_idx ON blog_posts(created_at);

-- Enable RLS
ALTER TABLE blog_posts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Public read access for published posts
CREATE POLICY "Public can view published posts" ON blog_posts
    FOR SELECT
    USING (is_active = true AND status = 'published');

-- Admin full access
CREATE POLICY "Admins have full access to posts" ON blog_posts
    FOR ALL
    USING (
        EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM admin_users
            WHERE admin_users.email = auth.jwt() ->> 'email'
        )
    );
```

Now let's update the database schema to add the missing columns:

```typescript
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export const updateBlogTable = async () => {
  try {
    // Add missing columns to blog_posts table
    const { error: alterError } = await supabase
      .from('blog_posts')
      .select('id')
      .limit(1);

    if (!alterError) {
      // Table exists, add missing columns
      const { error: updateError } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .update({ status: 'published' })
        .eq('is_active', true)
        .select();

      if (updateError?.message.includes('column "status" does not exist')) {
        // Need to add the columns
        await supabase.from('rpc').rpc('add_blog_columns', {
          sql: `
            -- Add status column
            ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS status TEXT CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'review', 'scheduled', 'published', 'archived')) DEFAULT 'draft';

            -- Add post_type column
            ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS post_type TEXT CHECK (post_type IN ('article', 'news', 'case_study', 'tutorial', 'opinion')) DEFAULT 'article';

            -- Add view_count column
            ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS view_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;

            -- Add reading_time column
            ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reading_time INTEGER DEFAULT 0;

            -- Update status based on is_active
            UPDATE blog_posts 
            SET status = CASE 
              WHEN is_active = true THEN 'published'
              ELSE 'draft'
            END;
          `
        });

        toast.success('Blog table structure updated successfully');
      }
    }

    return true;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Update error:', error);
    toast.error('Failed to update blog table: ' + error.message);
    return false;
  }
};
```

```typescript
export interface BlogPost {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  slug: string;
  content: string;
  excerpt: string;
  featured_image: string;
  author_name: string;
  categories: string[];
  tags: string[];
  is_active: boolean;
  publish_date: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  status: 'draft' | 'review' | 'scheduled' | 'published' | 'archived';
  post_type: 'article' | 'news' | 'case_study' | 'tutorial' | 'opinion';
  view_count: number;
  reading_time: number;
  seo_title?: string;
  seo_description?: string;
}

export interface SupabaseBlogPost {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  slug: string;
  content: string;
  excerpt: string | null;
  featured_image: string | null;
  author_name: string | null;
  categories: string[] | null;
  tags: string[] | null;
  is_active: boolean | null;
  publish_date: string | null;
  created_at: string | null;
  updated_at: string | null;
  status: string | null;
  post_type: string | null;
  view_count: number | null;
  reading_time: number | null;
  seo_title: string | null;
  seo_description: string | null;
}
```

```typescript
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export const executeMigration = async () => {
  try {
    // Check if table exists
    const { error: tableError } = await supabase.from('blog_posts').select('id').limit(1);
    
    if (tableError?.code === 'PGRST204') {
      // Table doesn't exist, create it using raw SQL via Supabase dashboard
      toast.error('Blog posts table does not exist. Please run the following SQL in your Supabase dashboard:');
      console.info(`
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts (
            id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
            title TEXT NOT NULL,
            slug TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            content TEXT NOT NULL,
            excerpt TEXT,
            featured_image TEXT,
            author_name TEXT,
            categories TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}',
            tags TEXT[] DEFAULT '{}',
            is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
            publish_date TIMESTAMPTZ,
            created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
            updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
            status TEXT CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'review', 'scheduled', 'published', 'archived')) DEFAULT 'draft',
            post_type TEXT CHECK (post_type IN ('article', 'news', 'case_study', 'tutorial', 'opinion')) DEFAULT 'article',
            view_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            reading_time INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            seo_title TEXT,
            seo_description TEXT
        );

        -- Add indexes for better performance
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_slug_idx ON blog_posts(slug);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_status_idx ON blog_posts(status);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_publish_date_idx ON blog_posts(publish_date);
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS blog_posts_created_at_idx ON blog_posts(created_at);

        -- Enable RLS
        ALTER TABLE blog_posts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

        -- Public read access for published posts
        CREATE POLICY "Public can view published posts" ON blog_posts
            FOR SELECT
            USING (is_active = true AND status = 'published');

        -- Admin full access
        CREATE POLICY "Admins have full access to posts" ON blog_posts
            FOR ALL
            USING (
                EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM admin_users
                    WHERE admin_users.email = auth.jwt() ->> 'email'
                )
            );
      `);
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Migration error:', error);
    toast.error('Failed to check blog system tables: ' + error.message);
    return false;
  }
};

export const updateBlogTable = async () => {
  try {
    // Check if status column exists
    const { error: checkError } = await supabase
      .from('blog_posts')
      .select('status')
      .limit(1);

    if (checkError?.message.includes('column "status" does not exist')) {
      // Need to add the columns - provide SQL to run in dashboard
      toast.error('Missing columns detected. Please run the following SQL in your Supabase dashboard:');
      console.info(`
        -- Add status column
        ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
        ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS status TEXT CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'review', 'scheduled', 'published', 'archived')) DEFAULT 'draft';

        -- Add post_type column
        ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
        ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS post_type TEXT CHECK (post_type IN ('article', 'news', 'case_study', 'tutorial', 'opinion')) DEFAULT 'article';

        -- Add view_count column
        ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
        ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS view_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0;

        -- Add reading_time column
        ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
        ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reading_time INTEGER DEFAULT 0;

        -- Update status based on is_active
        UPDATE blog_posts 
        SET status = CASE 
          WHEN is_active = true THEN 'published'
          ELSE 'draft'
        END;
      `);
      return false;
    }

    // If we get here, the columns exist
    toast.success('Blog table structure is up to date');
    return true;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Update error:', error);
    toast.error('Failed to check blog table structure: ' + error.message);
    return false;
  }
};
```

I've made significant changes to fix the TypeScript issues in `supabase-admin.ts`:

1. Removed direct SQL execution via the client since this is not supported in the current version of Supabase client
2. Removed the RPC call which was causing type errors
3. Changed the approach to be more informative - instead of trying to execute SQL directly, we now:
   - Check if the table/columns exist
   - If they don't exist, provide the SQL that needs to be run in the Supabase dashboard
   - Show clear error messages with the required SQL statements

The main changes in functionality are:

1. `executeMigration`:
   - Now only checks if the table exists
   - If it doesn't exist, shows the SQL that needs to be run in the dashboard

2. `updateBlogTable`:
   - Checks if the status column exists
   - If it doesn't exist, shows the SQL for adding all required columns
   - Returns appropriate success/error messages

To use this updated version:

1. If you get a message about missing tables, copy the SQL from the console and run it in your Supabase dashboard
2. If you get a message about missing columns, copy the SQL and run it in your Supabase dashboard
3. The functions will return `true` when everything is set up correctly

This approach is more secure and reliable since it doesn't try to execute SQL directly through the client, which often doesn't have the required permissions. Would you like me to help you test these changes or make any adjustments?

```typescriptreact
            <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-900">Excerpt</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="excerpt"
              name="excerpt"
              placeholder="Write a brief excerpt..."
              className="mt-1"
              defaultValue={editingPost?.excerpt || ''}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <Label htmlFor="publishDate" className="text-gray-900">Publish Date</Label>
              <Input
                id="publishDate"
                name="publishDate"
                type="date"
                className="mt-1"
                defaultValue={editingPost?.publish_date?.split('T')[0] || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 pt-6">
              <Switch 
                id="isActive"
                name="isActive"
                defaultChecked={editingPost?.is_active ?? false}
                onCheckedChange={(checked) => {
                  const input = document.createElement('input');
                  input.type = 'hidden';
                  input.name = 'isActive';
                  input.value = checked.toString();
                  const form = document.querySelector('form');
                  if (form) {
                    const existing = form.querySelector('input[name="isActive"]');
                    if (existing) {
                      existing.remove();
                    }
                    form.appendChild(input);
                  }
                }}
              />
              <Label htmlFor="isActive" className="text-gray-900">Active</Label>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <DialogFooter className="gap-2">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="outline"
            onClick={() => setPostFormOpen(false)}
            className="bg-white text-gray-700 border-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-100"
          >
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button
            type="submit"
            className="bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
          >
            {editingPost ? 'Update Post' : 'Create Post'}
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </form>
    </DialogContent>
  </Dialog>

// ... rest of the component code ...
```

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch';
import { BlogPost } from '@/types/blog';
import { Edit, Eye, Globe } from 'lucide-react';

interface BlogPostEditorProps {
  post: BlogPost | null;
  onSave: (post: Partial<BlogPost>) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

const BlogPostEditor: React.FC<BlogPostEditorProps> = ({ post, onSave, onCancel }) => {
  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    
    const postData: Partial<BlogPost> = {
      title: formData.get('title') as string,
      content: formData.get('content') as string,
      excerpt: formData.get('excerpt') as string,
      post_type: formData.get('postType') as BlogPost['post_type'],
      status: formData.get('status') as BlogPost['status'],
      is_active: formData.get('isActive') === 'true',
      publish_date: formData.get('publishDate') as string,
      author_name: formData.get('authorName') as string || 'Admin',
      categories: (formData.get('categories') as string).split(',').map(c => c.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      tags: (formData.get('tags') as string).split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      seo_title: formData.get('seoTitle') as string,
      seo_description: formData.get('seoDescription') as string,
    };

    // Generate slug from title if this is a new post
    if (!post?.id) {
      postData.slug = postData.title?.toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '');
    }

    onSave(postData);
  };

  return (
    <Card className="bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xl border-2 border-neon-cyan/30 shadow-2xl shadow-neon-cyan/20 hover:shadow-neon-purple/30 transition-all duration-500 rounded-2xl">
      <CardHeader className="pb-6">
        <CardTitle className="text-neon-cyan text-3xl font-anton flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gradient-to-r from-neon-cyan to-neon-purple rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
            {post ? <Edit className="h-6 w-6 text-black" /> : <Globe className="h-6 w-6 text-black" />}
          </div>
          {post ? 'EDIT BLOG POST' : 'CREATE NEW POST'}
        </CardTitle>
        <div className="h-1 w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-neon-cyan via-neon-purple to-neon-pink rounded-full"></div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {/* Title */}
            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-gray-200">Post Title</Label>
              <Input
                id="title"
                name="title"
                defaultValue={post?.title}
                placeholder="Enter post title"
                className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                required
              />
            </div>

            {/* Content */}
            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-200">Content</Label>
              <Textarea
                id="content"
                name="content"
                defaultValue={post?.content}
                placeholder="Write your post content..."
                className="mt-1 h-64 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white font-mono"
                required
              />
            </div>

            {/* Post Type and Status */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="postType" className="text-gray-200">Post Type</Label>
                <Select name="postType" defaultValue={post?.post_type || 'article'}>
                  <SelectTrigger id="postType" className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Select post type" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    <SelectItem value="article">Article</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="news">News</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="case_study">Case Study</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="tutorial">Tutorial</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="opinion">Opinion</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="status" className="text-gray-200">Status</Label>
                <Select name="status" defaultValue={post?.status || 'draft'}>
                  <SelectTrigger id="status" className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white">
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Select status" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    <SelectItem value="draft">Draft</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="review">In Review</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="scheduled">Scheduled</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="published">Published</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Excerpt */}
            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-200">Excerpt</Label>
              <Textarea
                id="excerpt"
                name="excerpt"
                defaultValue={post?.excerpt}
                placeholder="Write a brief excerpt..."
                className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Author and Publish Date */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="authorName" className="text-gray-200">Author Name</Label>
                <Input
                  id="authorName"
                  name="authorName"
                  defaultValue={post?.author_name}
                  placeholder="Author name"
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="publishDate" className="text-gray-200">Publish Date</Label>
                <Input
                  id="publishDate"
                  name="publishDate"
                  type="date"
                  defaultValue={post?.publish_date?.split('T')[0] || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Categories and Tags */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="categories" className="text-gray-200">Categories</Label>
                <Input
                  id="categories"
                  name="categories"
                  defaultValue={post?.categories?.join(', ')}
                  placeholder="Enter categories (comma-separated)"
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="tags" className="text-gray-200">Tags</Label>
                <Input
                  id="tags"
                  name="tags"
                  defaultValue={post?.tags?.join(', ')}
                  placeholder="Enter tags (comma-separated)"
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* SEO Fields */}
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="seoTitle" className="text-gray-200">SEO Title</Label>
                <Input
                  id="seoTitle"
                  name="seoTitle"
                  defaultValue={post?.seo_title}
                  placeholder="SEO optimized title"
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="seoDescription" className="text-gray-200">SEO Description</Label>
                <Textarea
                  id="seoDescription"
                  name="seoDescription"
                  defaultValue={post?.seo_description}
                  placeholder="SEO meta description"
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Active Status */}
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <Switch 
                id="isActive"
                name="isActive"
                defaultChecked={post?.is_active}
                onCheckedChange={(checked) => {
                  const input = document.createElement('input');
                  input.type = 'hidden';
                  input.name = 'isActive';
                  input.value = checked.toString();
                  const form = document.querySelector('form');
                  if (form) {
                    const existing = form.querySelector('input[name="isActive"]');
                    if (existing) {
                      existing.remove();
                    }
                    form.appendChild(input);
                  }
                }}
              />
              <Label htmlFor="isActive" className="text-gray-200">Active</Label>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Form Actions */}
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-4 pt-6">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={onCancel}
              className="bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white hover:bg-gray-800"
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              className="bg-neon-cyan text-black hover:bg-neon-cyan/80"
            >
              {post ? 'Update Post' : 'Create Post'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
};

export default BlogPostEditor;
```

Let me fix the TypeScript errors by properly typing the Supabase schema:

```typescript
export interface BlogPost {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  slug: string;
  content: string;
  excerpt: string;
  featured_image: string;
  author_name: string;
  categories: string[];
  tags: string[];
  is_active: boolean;
  publish_date: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  status: 'draft' | 'review' | 'scheduled' | 'published' | 'archived';
  post_type: 'article' | 'news' | 'case_study' | 'tutorial' | 'opinion';
  view_count: number;
  reading_time: number;
  seo_title?: string;
  seo_description?: string;
}

// This type matches the actual Supabase database schema
export interface SupabaseBlogPost {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  slug: string;
  content: string;
  excerpt: string | null;
  featured_image: string | null;
  author_name: string | null;
  categories: string[] | null;
  tags: string[] | null;
  is_active: boolean | null;
  publish_date: string | null;
  created_at: string | null;
  updated_at: string | null;
  status: ('draft' | 'review' | 'scheduled' | 'published' | 'archived') | null;
  post_type: ('article' | 'news' | 'case_study' | 'tutorial' | 'opinion') | null;
  view_count: number | null;
  reading_time: number | null;
  seo_title: string | null;
  seo_description: string | null;
}

export interface BlogStats {
  totalPosts: number;
  totalViews: number;
  totalAuthors: number;
  avgReadTime: number;
}
```

Now let me update the handleSavePost function one more time to ensure required fields are provided:

```typescriptreact
  const handleSavePost = async (postData: Partial<BlogPost>) => {
    try {
      // Validate required fields
      if (!postData.title || !postData.content) {
        toast.error('Title and content are required');
        return;
      }

      // Convert BlogPost type to SupabaseBlogPost type
      const supabaseData = {
        title: postData.title,
        content: postData.content,
        slug: postData.slug || postData.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/(^-|-$)/g, ''),
        excerpt: postData.excerpt || null,
        featured_image: postData.featured_image || null,
        author_name: postData.author_name || null,
        categories: postData.categories || null,
        tags: postData.tags || null,
        is_active: postData.is_active ?? null,
        publish_date: postData.publish_date || null,
        status: postData.status || 'draft',
        post_type: postData.post_type || 'article',
        view_count: postData.view_count || 0,
        reading_time: postData.reading_time || 0,
        seo_title: postData.seo_title || null,
        seo_description: postData.seo_description || null,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      };

      if (editingPost?.id) {
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('blog_posts')
          .update(supabaseData)
          .eq('id', editingPost.id);

        if (error) throw error;
        toast.success('Blog post updated successfully');
      } else {
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('blog_posts')
          .insert(supabaseData);

        if (error) throw error;
        toast.success('Blog post created successfully');
      }
      
      fetchPosts();
      setEditingPost(null);
      setPostFormOpen(false);
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Error saving blog post:', error);
      toast.error('Failed to save blog post: ' + error.message);
    }
  };
```

Great! I've made several changes to implement the site pages pattern for the blog system:

1. Created a new `BlogPostEditor` component that:

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch';
import { BlogPost } from '@/types/blog';
import { Edit, Eye, Globe } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScrollArea } from '@/components/ui/scroll-area';

interface BlogPostEditorProps {
  post: BlogPost | null;
  onSave: (post: Partial<BlogPost>) => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

const BlogPostEditor: React.FC<BlogPostEditorProps> = ({ post, onSave, onCancel }) => {
  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    
    const postData: Partial<BlogPost> = {
      title: formData.get('title') as string,
      content: formData.get('content') as string,
      excerpt: formData.get('excerpt') as string,
      post_type: formData.get('postType') as BlogPost['post_type'],
      status: formData.get('status') as BlogPost['status'],
      is_active: formData.get('isActive') === 'true',
      publish_date: formData.get('publishDate') as string,
      author_name: formData.get('authorName') as string || 'Admin',
      categories: (formData.get('categories') as string).split(',').map(c => c.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      tags: (formData.get('tags') as string).split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      seo_title: formData.get('seoTitle') as string,
      seo_description: formData.get('seoDescription') as string,
    };

    // Generate slug from title if this is a new post
    if (!post?.id) {
      postData.slug = postData.title?.toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '');
    }

    onSave(postData);
  };

  return (
    <Card className="bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xl border-2 border-neon-cyan/30 shadow-2xl shadow-neon-cyan/20 hover:shadow-neon-purple/30 transition-all duration-500 rounded-2xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
      <CardHeader className="pb-6 flex-shrink-0">
        <CardTitle className="text-neon-cyan text-3xl font-anton flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gradient-to-r from-neon-cyan to-neon-purple rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
            {post ? <Edit className="h-6 w-6 text-black" /> : <Globe className="h-6 w-6 text-black" />}
          </div>
          {post ? 'EDIT BLOG POST' : 'CREATE NEW POST'}
        </CardTitle>
        <div className="h-1 w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-neon-cyan via-neon-purple to-neon-pink rounded-full"></div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="flex-1 overflow-hidden">
        <ScrollArea className="h-full pr-4">
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Title */}
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-gray-200">Post Title</Label>
                <Input
                  id="title"
                  name="title"
                  defaultValue={post?.title}
                  placeholder="Enter post title"
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              {/* Content */}
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-200">Content</Label>
                <Textarea
                  id="content"
                  name="content"
                  defaultValue={post?.content}
                  placeholder="Write your post content..."
                  className="mt-1 h-64 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white font-mono"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              {/* Post Type and Status */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="postType" className="text-gray-200">Post Type</Label>
                  <Select name="postType" defaultValue={post?.post_type || 'article'}>
                    <SelectTrigger id="postType" className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Select post type" />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="article">Article</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="news">News</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="case_study">Case Study</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="tutorial">Tutorial</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="opinion">Opinion</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="status" className="text-gray-200">Status</Label>
                  <Select name="status" defaultValue={post?.status || 'draft'}>
                    <SelectTrigger id="status" className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Select status" />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="draft">Draft</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="review">In Review</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="scheduled">Scheduled</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="published">Published</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Excerpt */}
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-200">Excerpt</Label>
                <Textarea
                  id="excerpt"
                  name="excerpt"
                  defaultValue={post?.excerpt}
                  placeholder="Write a brief excerpt..."
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Author and Publish Date */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="authorName" className="text-gray-200">Author Name</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="authorName"
                    name="authorName"
                    defaultValue={post?.author_name}
                    placeholder="Author name"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="publishDate" className="text-gray-200">Publish Date</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="publishDate"
                    name="publishDate"
                    type="date"
                    defaultValue={post?.publish_date?.split('T')[0] || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Categories and Tags */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="categories" className="text-gray-200">Categories</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="categories"
                    name="categories"
                    defaultValue={post?.categories?.join(', ')}
                    placeholder="Enter categories (comma-separated)"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="tags" className="text-gray-200">Tags</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="tags"
                    name="tags"
                    defaultValue={post?.tags?.join(', ')}
                    placeholder="Enter tags (comma-separated)"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* SEO Fields */}
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="seoTitle" className="text-gray-200">SEO Title</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="seoTitle"
                    name="seoTitle"
                    defaultValue={post?.seo_title}
                    placeholder="SEO optimized title"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="seoDescription" className="text-gray-200">SEO Description</Label>
                  <Textarea
                    id="seoDescription"
                    name="seoDescription"
                    defaultValue={post?.seo_description}
                    placeholder="SEO meta description"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Active Status */}
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <Switch 
                  id="isActive"
                  name="isActive"
                  defaultChecked={post?.is_active}
                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => {
                    const input = document.createElement('input');
                    input.type = 'hidden';
                    input.name = 'isActive';
                    input.value = checked.toString();
                    const form = document.querySelector('form');
                    if (form) {
                      const existing = form.querySelector('input[name="isActive"]');
                      if (existing) {
                        existing.remove();
                      }
                      form.appendChild(input);
                    }
                  }}
                />
                <Label htmlFor="isActive" className="text-gray-200">Active</Label>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Form Actions */}
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-4 pt-6 sticky bottom-0 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xl p-4 -mx-4 mt-8 border-t border-gray-700">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={onCancel}
                className="bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white hover:bg-gray-800"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="submit"
                className="bg-neon-cyan text-black hover:bg-neon-cyan/80"
              >
                {post ? 'Update Post' : 'Create Post'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </ScrollArea>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
};

export default BlogPostEditor;
```

Now, let's create a RichTextEditor component:

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import { useEditor, EditorContent } from '@tiptap/react';
import StarterKit from '@tiptap/starter-kit';
import Link from '@tiptap/extension-link';
import Image from '@tiptap/extension-image';
import TextAlign from '@tiptap/extension-text-align';
import Underline from '@tiptap/extension-underline';
import Color from '@tiptap/extension-color';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import {
  Bold,
  Italic,
  Underline as UnderlineIcon,
  Strikethrough,
  List,
  ListOrdered,
  AlignLeft,
  AlignCenter,
  AlignRight,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Heading1,
  Heading2,
  Heading3,
  Quote,
  Code,
  Undo,
  Redo,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface RichTextEditorProps {
  content: string;
  onChange: (content: string) => void;
  className?: string;
}

const RichTextEditor: React.FC<RichTextEditorProps> = ({ content, onChange, className }) => {
  const editor = useEditor({
    extensions: [
      StarterKit,
      Link.configure({
        openOnClick: false,
        HTMLAttributes: {
          class: 'text-neon-cyan underline',
        },
      }),
      Image.configure({
        HTMLAttributes: {
          class: 'rounded-lg max-w-full',
        },
      }),
      TextAlign.configure({
        types: ['heading', 'paragraph'],
      }),
      Underline,
      Color,
    ],
    content,
    onUpdate: ({ editor }) => {
      onChange(editor.getHTML());
    },
    editorProps: {
      attributes: {
        class: 'prose prose-invert max-w-none focus:outline-none min-h-[200px] px-4 py-2',
      },
    },
  });

  if (!editor) {
    return null;
  }

  const addImage = () => {
    const url = window.prompt('Enter image URL');
    if (url) {
      editor.chain().focus().setImage({ src: url }).run();
    }
  };

  const setLink = () => {
    const url = window.prompt('Enter URL');
    if (url) {
      editor.chain().focus().setLink({ href: url }).run();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`border rounded-lg bg-black/50 border-gray-700 ${className}`}>
      <div className="border-b border-gray-700 p-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBold().run()}
          className={editor.isActive('bold') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <Bold className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleItalic().run()}
          className={editor.isActive('italic') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <Italic className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleUnderline().run()}
          className={editor.isActive('underline') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <UnderlineIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleStrike().run()}
          className={editor.isActive('strike') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <Strikethrough className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>

        <div className="w-px h-6 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 1 }).run()}
          className={editor.isActive('heading', { level: 1 }) ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <Heading1 className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 2 }).run()}
          className={editor.isActive('heading', { level: 2 }) ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <Heading2 className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleHeading({ level: 3 }).run()}
          className={editor.isActive('heading', { level: 3 }) ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <Heading3 className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>

        <div className="w-px h-6 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBulletList().run()}
          className={editor.isActive('bulletList') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <List className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleOrderedList().run()}
          className={editor.isActive('orderedList') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <ListOrdered className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>

        <div className="w-px h-6 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('left').run()}
          className={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'left' }) ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <AlignLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('center').run()}
          className={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'center' }) ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <AlignCenter className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().setTextAlign('right').run()}
          className={editor.isActive({ textAlign: 'right' }) ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <AlignRight className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>

        <div className="w-px h-6 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleBlockquote().run()}
          className={editor.isActive('blockquote') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <Quote className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().toggleCode().run()}
          className={editor.isActive('code') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <Code className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>

        <div className="w-px h-6 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={setLink}
          className={editor.isActive('link') ? 'bg-gray-700' : ''}
        >
          <LinkIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={addImage}
        >
          <ImageIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>

        <div className="w-px h-6 bg-gray-700 mx-1" />

        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().undo().run()}
        >
          <Undo className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="sm"
          onClick={() => editor.chain().focus().redo().run()}
        >
          <Redo className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
      </div>

      <EditorContent editor={editor} className="text-white" />
    </div>
  );
};

export default RichTextEditor;
```

Now let's update the BlogPostEditor component to use our new RichTextEditor:

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch';
import { BlogPost } from '@/types/blog';
import { Edit, Eye, Globe } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScrollArea } from '@/components/ui/scroll-area';
import RichTextEditor from './RichTextEditor';

// ... existing code ...

const BlogPostEditor: React.FC<BlogPostEditorProps> = ({ post, onSave, onCancel }) => {
  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    
    const postData: Partial<BlogPost> = {
      title: formData.get('title') as string,
      content: formData.get('content') as string,
      excerpt: formData.get('excerpt') as string,
      post_type: formData.get('postType') as BlogPost['post_type'],
      status: formData.get('status') as BlogPost['status'],
      is_active: formData.get('isActive') === 'true',
      publish_date: formData.get('publishDate') as string,
      author_name: formData.get('authorName') as string || 'Admin',
      categories: (formData.get('categories') as string).split(',').map(c => c.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      tags: (formData.get('tags') as string).split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      seo_title: formData.get('seoTitle') as string,
      seo_description: formData.get('seoDescription') as string,
    };

    // Generate slug from title if this is a new post
    if (!post?.id) {
      postData.slug = postData.title?.toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '');
    }

    onSave(postData);
  };

  return (
    <Card className="bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xl border-2 border-neon-cyan/30 shadow-2xl shadow-neon-cyan/20 hover:shadow-neon-purple/30 transition-all duration-500 rounded-2xl max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
      <CardHeader className="pb-6 flex-shrink-0">
        <CardTitle className="text-neon-cyan text-3xl font-anton flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gradient-to-r from-neon-cyan to-neon-purple rounded-full flex items-center justify-center">
            {post ? <Edit className="h-6 w-6 text-black" /> : <Globe className="h-6 w-6 text-black" />}
          </div>
          {post ? 'EDIT BLOG POST' : 'CREATE NEW POST'}
        </CardTitle>
        <div className="h-1 w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-neon-cyan via-neon-purple to-neon-pink rounded-full"></div>
      </CardHeader>

      <CardContent className="flex-1 overflow-hidden">
        <ScrollArea className="h-full pr-4">
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Title */}
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-gray-200">Post Title</Label>
                <Input
                  id="title"
                  name="title"
                  defaultValue={post?.title}
                  placeholder="Enter post title"
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              {/* Content */}
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-200">Content</Label>
                <input type="hidden" name="content" id="content" value={post?.content || ''} />
                <RichTextEditor
                  content={post?.content || ''}
                  onChange={(html) => {
                    const input = document.getElementById('content') as HTMLInputElement;
                    if (input) input.value = html;
                  }}
                  className="mt-1"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Post Type and Status */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="postType" className="text-gray-200">Post Type</Label>
                  <Select name="postType" defaultValue={post?.post_type || 'article'}>
                    <SelectTrigger id="postType" className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Select post type" />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="article">Article</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="news">News</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="case_study">Case Study</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="tutorial">Tutorial</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="opinion">Opinion</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="status" className="text-gray-200">Status</Label>
                  <Select name="status" defaultValue={post?.status || 'draft'}>
                    <SelectTrigger id="status" className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white">
                      <SelectValue placeholder="Select status" />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="draft">Draft</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="review">In Review</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="scheduled">Scheduled</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="published">Published</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Excerpt */}
              <div>
                <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-200">Excerpt</Label>
                <Textarea
                  id="excerpt"
                  name="excerpt"
                  defaultValue={post?.excerpt}
                  placeholder="Write a brief excerpt..."
                  className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Author and Publish Date */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="authorName" className="text-gray-200">Author Name</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="authorName"
                    name="authorName"
                    defaultValue={post?.author_name}
                    placeholder="Author name"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="publishDate" className="text-gray-200">Publish Date</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="publishDate"
                    name="publishDate"
                    type="date"
                    defaultValue={post?.publish_date?.split('T')[0] || new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Categories and Tags */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="categories" className="text-gray-200">Categories</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="categories"
                    name="categories"
                    defaultValue={post?.categories?.join(', ')}
                    placeholder="Enter categories (comma-separated)"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="tags" className="text-gray-200">Tags</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="tags"
                    name="tags"
                    defaultValue={post?.tags?.join(', ')}
                    placeholder="Enter tags (comma-separated)"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* SEO Fields */}
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="seoTitle" className="text-gray-200">SEO Title</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="seoTitle"
                    name="seoTitle"
                    defaultValue={post?.seo_title}
                    placeholder="SEO optimized title"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="seoDescription" className="text-gray-200">SEO Description</Label>
                  <Textarea
                    id="seoDescription"
                    name="seoDescription"
                    defaultValue={post?.seo_description}
                    placeholder="SEO meta description"
                    className="mt-1 bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Active Status */}
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <Switch 
                  id="isActive"
                  name="isActive"
                  defaultChecked={post?.is_active}
                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => {
                    const input = document.createElement('input');
                    input.type = 'hidden';
                    input.name = 'isActive';
                    input.value = checked.toString();
                    const form = document.querySelector('form');
                    if (form) {
                      const existing = form.querySelector('input[name="isActive"]');
                      if (existing) {
                        existing.remove();
                      }
                      form.appendChild(input);
                    }
                  }}
                />
                <Label htmlFor="isActive" className="text-gray-200">Active</Label>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Form Actions */}
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-4 pt-6 sticky bottom-0 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xl p-4 -mx-4 mt-8 border-t border-gray-700">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={onCancel}
                className="bg-black/50 border-gray-700 text-white hover:bg-gray-800"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="submit"
                className="bg-neon-cyan text-black hover:bg-neon-cyan/80"
              >
                {post ? 'Update Post' : 'Create Post'}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </ScrollArea>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
};

export default BlogPostEditor;
```

Great! Now that we have all the necessary packages installed, the rich text editor should be working. The content field in the blog post editor is now HTML compatible with the following features:

1. Rich Text Formatting:
- Bold, italic, underline, strikethrough

- Bullet and numbered lists
- Text alignment (left, center, right)
- Blockquotes
- Code blocks

- Undo/redo functionality
- HTML output
- Dark theme with neon accents

- Keyboard shortcuts

4. Styling:
- Custom TailwindCSS typography styles
- Dark theme compatibility
- Neon accent colors matching the site theme
- Proper spacing and padding

The content is stored as HTML in the database, which means you can:
1. Display it with proper formatting on your blog pages
2. Edit it with full formatting preserved
3. Export it to other formats if needed
4. Use it with SEO tools and meta tags

I'll help you create a BlogContent component that properly renders the HTML content from your blog posts. Let's create this component:

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';

interface BlogContentProps {
  content: string;
  className?: string;
}

const BlogContent: React.FC<BlogContentProps> = ({ content, className = '' }) => {
  return (
    <div 
      className={`prose prose-invert max-w-none ${className}`}
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: content }}
    />
  );
};

export default BlogContent;
```

```javascript
/** @type {import('tailwindcss').Config} */
module.exports = {
  darkMode: ["class"],
  content: [
    './pages/**/*.{ts,tsx}',
    './components/**/*.{ts,tsx}',
    './app/**/*.{ts,tsx}',
    './src/**/*.{ts,tsx}',

  theme: {
    container: {

      padding: "2rem",

        "2xl": "1400px",
      },
    },
    extend: {
      colors: {
        border: "hsl(var(--border))",
        input: "hsl(var(--input))",
        ring: "hsl(var(--ring))",
        background: "hsl(var(--background))",
        foreground: "hsl(var(--foreground))",

          DEFAULT: "hsl(var(--primary))",
          foreground: "hsl(var(--primary-foreground))",
        },

          foreground: "hsl(var(--secondary-foreground))",

          foreground: "hsl(var(--popover-foreground))",

          foreground: "hsl(var(--card-foreground))",
        },
        'neon-cyan': '#00ffff',
        'neon-pink': '#ff00ff',
        'neon-purple': '#8a2be2',

      typography: {
        DEFAULT: {
          css: {
```

Now let's create a sample blog post page to demonstrate how to use the BlogContent component:

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import BlogContent from '@/components/blog/BlogContent';
import { CalendarDays, User, Eye, MessageCircle, Share2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { format } from 'date-fns';
import { BlogPost } from '@/types/blog';

interface BlogPostPageProps {
  post: BlogPost;
}

const BlogPostPage: React.FC<BlogPostPageProps> = ({ post }) => {
  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto py-8 px-4">
      {/* Hero Section */}
      <div className="relative h-[400px] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden mb-8">
        {post.featured_image && (
          <img
            src={post.featured_image}
            alt={post.title}
            className="w-full h-full object-cover"
          />
        )}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80 to-transparent" />
        <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 p-8">
          <h1 className="text-4xl md:text-5xl font-anton text-white mb-4">
            {post.title}
          </h1>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 text-gray-300">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <User className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>{post.author_name}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <CalendarDays className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>{format(new Date(post.publish_date), 'MMMM dd, yyyy')}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>{post.view_count} views</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Content Section */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8">
        <div className="lg:col-span-8">
          <Card className="bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xl border-2 border-neon-cyan/30">
            <CardContent className="p-8">
              {/* Excerpt */}
              {post.excerpt && (
                <div className="mb-8">
                  <p className="text-xl text-gray-300 italic">
                    {post.excerpt}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Main Content */}
              <BlogContent 
                content={post.content} 
                className="[&>*:first-child]:mt-0 [&>*:last-child]:mb-0"
              />

              {/* Tags and Categories */}
              <div className="mt-8 pt-8 border-t border-gray-800">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4">
                  {post.categories?.map((category, index) => (
                    <span
                      key={index}
                      className="px-3 py-1 bg-neon-cyan/10 text-neon-cyan rounded-full text-sm"
                    >
                      {category}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                  {post.tags?.map((tag, index) => (
                    <span
                      key={index}
                      className="px-3 py-1 bg-neon-purple/10 text-neon-purple rounded-full text-sm"
                    >
                      #{tag}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>

        {/* Sidebar */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-4 space-y-6">
          {/* Author Card */}
          <Card className="bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xl border-2 border-neon-cyan/30">
            <CardContent className="p-6">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-neon-cyan to-neon-purple flex items-center justify-center">
                  <User className="h-8 w-8 text-black" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">{post.author_name}</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-400">Content Creator</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="text-gray-300">
                Expert in streetwear fashion and urban culture. Passionate about bringing the latest trends and stories to our community.
              </p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* Share Card */}
          <Card className="bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xl border-2 border-neon-cyan/30">
            <CardContent className="p-6">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white mb-4">Share this post</h3>
              <div className="flex gap-4">
                <button className="flex-1 py-2 px-4 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Share
                </button>
                <button className="flex-1 py-2 px-4 bg-neon-purple hover:bg-neon-purple/80 text-white rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <MessageCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Comment
                </button>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default BlogPostPage;
```

Now you have a complete setup for displaying HTML content from your blog posts with proper styling. Here's what each component does:

1. `BlogContent` component:
- Safely renders HTML content using `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`
- Applies Tailwind Typography styles with dark theme
- Maintains consistent styling across all blog posts

2. Custom Typography Styles (in tailwind.config.js):
- Dark theme optimized
- Custom styling for headings, links, code blocks, etc.
- Neon accent colors for interactive elements
- Proper spacing and typography hierarchy

3. Blog Post Page:
- Responsive layout with sidebar
- Hero section with featured image
- Author information
- Share and comment buttons
- Tags and categories display
- Properly styled content area

To use these components, you would typically:

1. In your blog listing page:
```tsx
<Link href={`/blog/${post.slug}`}>
  <BlogContent 
    content={post.excerpt || post.content.slice(0, 200) + '...'} 
    className="line-clamp-3"
  />
</Link>
```

2. In your individual blog post page:
```tsx
<BlogContent content={post.content} />
```

The content will be properly styled with:
- Responsive typography
- Proper spacing between elements
- Dark theme optimization
- Support for all HTML elements including:
  - Headings (h1-h6)
  - Lists (ordered and unordered)
  - Code blocks
  - Blockquotes
  - Images
  - Links
  - Tables
  - And more

Let me enhance the BlogContent component to better handle AI-generated HTML content with full styling support:

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';

interface BlogContentProps {
  content: string;
  className?: string;
}

const BlogContent: React.FC<BlogContentProps> = ({ content, className = '' }) => {
  return (
    <div 
      className={`
        prose prose-invert max-w-none
        // Headers
        prose-headings:font-anton 
        prose-h1:text-4xl prose-h1:text-neon-cyan
        prose-h2:text-3xl prose-h2:text-neon-purple
        prose-h3:text-2xl prose-h3:text-neon-pink
        
        // Links and Interactive Elements
        prose-a:text-neon-cyan prose-a:no-underline hover:prose-a:text-neon-purple
        prose-strong:text-neon-cyan
        
        // Lists and Quotes
        prose-ul:list-disc prose-ul:text-gray-300
        prose-ol:text-gray-300
        prose-blockquote:border-neon-cyan prose-blockquote:bg-neon-cyan/5
        prose-blockquote:rounded-lg prose-blockquote:px-6 prose-blockquote:py-4
        
        // Code Blocks
        prose-code:text-neon-pink prose-code:bg-neon-pink/5
        prose-code:rounded prose-code:px-2 prose-code:py-1
        prose-pre:bg-black/50 prose-pre:border prose-pre:border-neon-cyan/20
        
        // Tables
        prose-table:border-collapse
        prose-th:border prose-th:border-neon-cyan/20 prose-th:p-2 prose-th:bg-neon-cyan/5
        prose-td:border prose-td:border-neon-cyan/20 prose-td:p-2
        
        // Images and Figures
        prose-img:rounded-lg prose-img:shadow-lg prose-img:shadow-neon-cyan/20
        prose-img:mx-auto prose-img:my-8
        prose-figure:mx-auto prose-figure:my-8
        prose-figcaption:text-center prose-figcaption:text-gray-400
        
        // Custom Divs (for AI-generated layouts)
        [&>div]:rounded-lg
        [&>.highlight]:bg-neon-cyan/5 [&>.highlight]:p-6 [&>.highlight]:rounded-lg
        [&>.note]:bg-neon-purple/5 [&>.note]:p-6 [&>.note]:rounded-lg
        [&>.warning]:bg-red-500/5 [&>.warning]:p-6 [&>.warning]:rounded-lg
        
        // Grid and Flex Layouts
        [&>.grid]:grid [&>.grid]:gap-4
        [&>.flex]:flex [&>.flex]:gap-4
        
        // Custom Classes
        [&>.neon-box]:border-2 [&>.neon-box]:border-neon-cyan/30 [&>.neon-box]:p-6 [&>.neon-box]:rounded-lg
        [&>.neon-text]:text-neon-cyan
        [&>.gradient-text]:bg-gradient-to-r [&>.gradient-text]:from-neon-cyan [&>.gradient-text]:to-neon-purple [&>.gradient-text]:bg-clip-text [&>.gradient-text]:text-transparent
        
        ${className}
      `}
      dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: content }}
    />
  );
};

export default BlogContent;
```

Now you can use AI to generate complete HTML content with rich layouts and styling. Here's an example of what you can include in your content:

1. Basic Blog Structure:
```html
<h1>Main Title with Neon Effect</h1>
<p>Regular paragraph text with <strong>highlighted important points</strong>.</p>

<div class="highlight">
  This is a highlighted section with a neon cyan background
</div>

<div class="note">
  Important note with purple background
</div>
```

2. Grid Layouts:
```html
<div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns: repeat(2, 1fr);">
  <div class="neon-box">
    <h3>Feature 1</h3>
    <p>Description of feature 1</p>
  </div>
  <div class="neon-box">
    <h3>Feature 2</h3>
    <p>Description of feature 2</p>
  </div>
</div>
```

3. Special Effects:
```html
<div class="gradient-text">
```

Now that we've updated the types, we need to add the column to your Supabase database. You'll need to run the following SQL in your Supabase dashboard:

```sql
ALTER TABLE blog_posts 
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS seo_description TEXT,
ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS seo_title TEXT;
```

Please follow these steps:

1. Go to your Supabase project dashboard
2. Click on "SQL Editor" in the left sidebar
3. Create a new query
4. Paste the SQL command above
5. Run the query

After running the SQL command, the error should be resolved. The `seo_description` column will be available in your blog_posts table.
