```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Upload } from 'lucide-react';

interface BlogPostFormModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
}

const BlogPostFormModal: React.FC<BlogPostFormModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSuccess }) => {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: '',
    content: '',
    excerpt: '',
    author_name: '',
    status: 'draft',
    is_active: true,
    categories: '',
    tags: '',
    seo_title: '',
    seo_description: '',
    featured_image: ''
  });

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .insert([{
          ...formData,
          publish_date: new Date().toISOString(),
          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          categories: formData.categories.split(',').map(c => c.trim()).filter(Boolean),
          tags: formData.tags.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean)
        }]);

      if (error) throw error;

      toast.success('Blog post created successfully');
      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error('Failed to create post: ' + error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[800px] max-h-[90vh] bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 text-white overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
        <DialogHeader className="bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-900 to-gray-800 p-6 border-b border-gray-800">
          <DialogTitle className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Create New Blog Post
          </DialogTitle>
        </DialogHeader>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
          {/* Title */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-gray-300">Title</Label>
            <Input
              id="title"
              value={formData.title}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, title: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Enter post title"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Featured Image URL */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="featured-image-url" className="text-gray-300">Featured Image URL</Label>
            <Input
              id="featured-image-url"
              type="url"
              value={formData.featured_image}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, featured_image: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Enter image URL"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Content */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-300">Content</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="content"
              value={formData.content}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, content: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white min-h-[200px]"
              placeholder="Write your post content (HTML supported)"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Excerpt */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-300">Excerpt</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="excerpt"
              value={formData.excerpt}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, excerpt: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Brief description of the post"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            {/* Author */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="author" className="text-gray-300">Author</Label>
              <Input
                id="author"
                value={formData.author_name}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, author_name: e.target.value })}
                className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                placeholder="Author name"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Status */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label className="text-gray-300">Status</Label>
              <Select
                value={formData.status}
                onValueChange={(value) => setFormData({ ...formData, status: value })}
              >
                <SelectTrigger className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent className="bg-gray-900 border-gray-700">
                  <SelectItem value="draft">Draft</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="published">Published</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="archived">Archived</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>

            {/* Categories */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="categories" className="text-gray-300">Categories</Label>
              <Input
                id="categories"
                value={formData.categories}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, categories: e.target.value })}
                className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                placeholder="Comma-separated categories"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Tags */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="tags" className="text-gray-300">Tags</Label>
              <Input
                id="tags"
                value={formData.tags}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, tags: e.target.value })}
                className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                placeholder="Comma-separated tags"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* SEO Section */}
          <div className="space-y-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-800">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-white">SEO Settings</h3>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="seo_title" className="text-gray-300">SEO Title</Label>
                <Input
                  id="seo_title"
                  value={formData.seo_title}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, seo_title: e.target.value })}
                  className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  placeholder="SEO optimized title"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="seo_description" className="text-gray-300">SEO Description</Label>
                <Textarea
                  id="seo_description"
                  value={formData.seo_description}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, seo_description: e.target.value })}
                  className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  placeholder="SEO meta description"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Active Status */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-gray-800">
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-white">Post Visibility</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Make this post visible on the site</p>
            </div>
            <Switch
              checked={formData.is_active}
              onCheckedChange={(checked) => setFormData({ ...formData, is_active: checked })}
              className="data-[state=checked]:bg-neon-cyan"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Form Actions */}
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-6">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="bg-gray-800/50 text-white hover:bg-gray-700/50 border-gray-700"
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="bg-neon-cyan text-gray-900 hover:bg-neon-cyan/90"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <>
                  <Upload className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                  Creating...
                </>
              ) : 'Create Post'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

export default BlogPostFormModal;
```

```typescriptreact
        <DialogHeader className="bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-900 to-gray-800 p-6 border-b border-gray-800">
```

```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Upload } from 'lucide-react';

interface BlogPostFormModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
}

const BlogPostFormModal: React.FC<BlogPostFormModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSuccess }) => {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: '',
    content: '',
    excerpt: '',
    author_name: '',
    status: 'draft',
    is_active: true,
    categories: '',
    tags: '',
    seo_title: '',
    seo_description: '',
    featured_image: ''
  });

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .insert([{
          ...formData,
          publish_date: new Date().toISOString(),
          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          categories: formData.categories.split(',').map(c => c.trim()).filter(Boolean),
          tags: formData.tags.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean)
        }]);

      if (error) throw error;

      toast.success('Blog post created successfully');
      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error('Failed to create post: ' + error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[800px] max-h-[90vh] bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 text-white overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
        <DialogHeader className="bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-900 to-gray-800 p-6 border-b border-gray-800">
          <DialogTitle className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Create New Blog Post
          </DialogTitle>
        </DialogHeader>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
          {/* Title */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-gray-300">Title</Label>
            <Input
              id="title"
              value={formData.title}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, title: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Enter post title"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Featured Image URL */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="featured-image-url" className="text-gray-300">Featured Image URL</Label>
            <Input
              id="featured-image-url"
              type="url"
              value={formData.featured_image}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, featured_image: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Enter image URL"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Content */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-300">Content</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="content"
              value={formData.content}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, content: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white min-h-[200px]"
              placeholder="Write your post content (HTML supported)"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Excerpt */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-300">Excerpt</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="excerpt"
              value={formData.excerpt}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, excerpt: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Brief description of the post"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            {/* Author */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="author" className="text-gray-300">Author</Label>
              <Input
                id="author"
                value={formData.author_name}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, author_name: e.target.value })}
                className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                placeholder="Author name"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Status */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label className="text-gray-300">Status</Label>
              <Select
                value={formData.status}
                onValueChange={(value) => setFormData({ ...formData, status: value })}
              >
                <SelectTrigger className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent className="bg-gray-900 border-gray-700">
                  <SelectItem value="draft">Draft</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="published">Published</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="archived">Archived</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>

            {/* Categories */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="categories" className="text-gray-300">Categories</Label>
              <Input
                id="categories"
                value={formData.categories}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, categories: e.target.value })}
                className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                placeholder="Comma-separated categories"
              />

            {/* Tags */}
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <Label htmlFor="tags" className="text-gray-300">Tags</Label>
              <Input
                id="tags"
                value={formData.tags}
                onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, tags: e.target.value })}
                className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                placeholder="Comma-separated tags"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* SEO Section */}
          <div className="space-y-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-800">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-white">SEO Settings</h3>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-2">

              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Active Status */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-4 border-t border-gray-800">
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-white">Post Visibility</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">Make this post visible on the site</p>
            </div>
            <Switch
              checked={formData.is_active}
              onCheckedChange={(checked) => setFormData({ ...formData, is_active: checked })}
              className="data-[state=checked]:bg-neon-cyan"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Form Actions */}
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-6">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="bg-gray-800/50 text-white hover:bg-gray-700/50 border-gray-700"
            >
              Cancel

            <Button

              className="bg-neon-cyan text-gray-900 hover:bg-neon-cyan/90"

              {loading ? (

                  Creating...

              ) : 'Create Post'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
};
```

```typescriptreact
            <Label htmlFor="featured-image-url" className="text-gray-300">Featured Image URL</Label>
            <Input
              id="featured-image-url"
              type="url"
              value={formData.featured_image}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, featured_image: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Enter image URL"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Rest of the form fields remain the same */}
          {/* ... existing code ... */}
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

export default BlogPostFormModal;
```

2. Added a description text to make the dialog more accessible

```typescriptreact
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogDescription } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch';
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from '@/components/ui/select';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { Upload, Image as ImageIcon, FileText, Tags, User, Settings, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card, CardContent } from '@/components/ui/card';
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs';

interface BlogPost {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  content: string;
  excerpt: string;
  author_name: string;
  status: 'draft' | 'published' | 'archived';
  is_active: boolean;
  categories: string[];
  tags: string[];
  seo_title: string;
  seo_description: string;
  featured_image_url: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
  publish_date: string;
}

interface BlogPostFormModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
  editingPost?: BlogPost | null;
}

const BlogPostFormModal: React.FC<BlogPostFormModalProps> = ({ 
  isOpen, 
  onClose, 
  onSuccess,
  editingPost 
}) => {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('content');
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: '',
    content: '',
    excerpt: '',
    author_name: '',
    status: 'draft' as 'draft' | 'published' | 'archived',
    is_active: true,
    categories: '',
    tags: '',
    seo_title: '',
    seo_description: '',
    featured_image_url: ''
  });

  // Initialize form data when editing a post
  useEffect(() => {
    if (editingPost) {
      setFormData({
        title: editingPost.title || '',
        content: editingPost.content || '',
        excerpt: editingPost.excerpt || '',
        author_name: editingPost.author_name || '',
        status: editingPost.status || 'draft',
        is_active: editingPost.is_active,
        categories: editingPost.categories?.join(', ') || '',
        tags: editingPost.tags?.join(', ') || '',
        seo_title: editingPost.seo_title || '',
        seo_description: editingPost.seo_description || '',
        featured_image_url: editingPost.featured_image_url || ''
      });
    } else {
      // Reset form when creating new post
      setFormData({
        title: '',
        content: '',
        excerpt: '',
        author_name: '',
        status: 'draft',
        is_active: true,
        categories: '',
        tags: '',
        seo_title: '',
        seo_description: '',
        featured_image_url: ''
      });
    }
  }, [editingPost, isOpen]);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const postData = {
        title: formData.title,
        content: formData.content,
        excerpt: formData.excerpt,
        author_name: formData.author_name,
        status: formData.status,
        is_active: formData.is_active,
        categories: formData.categories.split(',').map(c => c.trim()).filter(Boolean),
        tags: formData.tags.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean),
        seo_title: formData.seo_title,
        seo_description: formData.seo_description,
        featured_image_url: formData.featured_image_url,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
      };

      if (editingPost) {
        // Update existing post
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('blog_posts')
          .update(postData)
          .eq('id', editingPost.id);

        if (error) throw error;
        toast.success('Blog post updated successfully');
      } else {
        // Create new post
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('blog_posts')
          .insert([{
            ...postData,
            publish_date: formData.status === 'published' ? new Date().toISOString() : null,
            created_at: new Date().toISOString()
          }]);

        if (error) throw error;
        toast.success('Blog post created successfully');
      }

      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Error saving blog post:', error);
      toast.error(`Failed to ${editingPost ? 'update' : 'create'} post: ${error.message}`);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const validateImageUrl = (url: string): boolean => {
    if (!url) return true; // Empty URL is valid
    try {
      new URL(url);
      return url.match(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif|webp)$/i) !== null;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[900px] max-h-[90vh] bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 text-white overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
        <DialogHeader className="bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-900 to-gray-800 p-6 border-b border-gray-800">
          <DialogTitle className="text-xl font-semibold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <FileText className="h-5 w-5" />
            {editingPost ? 'Edit Blog Post' : 'Create New Blog Post'}
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription className="text-sm text-gray-400">
            {editingPost ? 'Update your blog post details below' : 'Fill in the details below to create a new blog post'}
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        <Tabs value={activeTab} onValueChange={setActiveTab} className="flex-1 overflow-hidden">
          <div className="border-b border-gray-800 px-6">
            <TabsList className="bg-gray-800/50">
              <TabsTrigger value="content" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gray-700">
                <FileText className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Content
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="media" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gray-700">
                <ImageIcon className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Media
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="metadata" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gray-700">
                <Tags className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                Metadata
              </TabsTrigger>
              <TabsTrigger value="seo" className="data-[state=active]:bg-gray-700">
                <Search className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                SEO
              </TabsTrigger>
            </TabsList>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
            <TabsContent value="content" className="p-6 space-y-6">
              {/* Title */}
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-gray-300">Title</Label>
                <Input
                  id="title"
                  value={formData.title}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, title: e.target.value })}
                  className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  placeholder="Enter post title"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              {/* Content */}
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-300">Content</Label>
                <Textarea
                  id="content"
                  value={formData.content}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, content: e.target.value })}
                  className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white min-h-[300px]"
                  placeholder="Write your post content (HTML supported)"
                  required
                />
              </div>

              {/* Excerpt */}
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-300">Excerpt</Label>
                <Textarea
                  id="excerpt"
                  value={formData.excerpt}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, excerpt: e.target.value })}
                  className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                  placeholder="Brief description of the post"
                />
              </div>
            </TabsContent>

            <TabsContent value="media" className="p-6 space-y-6">
              <Card className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700">
                <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
                    <ImageIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-gray-400" />
                    <h3 className="text-lg font-medium text-white">Featured Image</h3>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <Label htmlFor="featured-image-url" className="text-gray-300">
                        Featured Image URL
                      </Label>
                      <Input
                        id="featured-image-url"
                        type="url"
                        value={formData.featured_image_url}
                        onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, featured_image_url: e.target.value })}
                        className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                        placeholder="https://example.com/image.jpg"
                      />
                      {formData.featured_image_url && !validateImageUrl(formData.featured_image_url) && (
                        <p className="text-red-400 text-sm mt-1">
                          Please enter a valid image URL (ending in .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .gif, or .webp)
                        </p>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {formData.featured_image_url && validateImageUrl(formData.featured_image_url) && (
                      <div className="relative aspect-video rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-700">
                        <img
                          src={formData.featured_image_url}
                          alt="Featured image preview"
                          className="object-cover w-full h-full"
                          onError={(e) => {
                            e.currentTarget.src = 'https://placehold.co/600x400/1f2937/ffffff?text=Image+Not+Found';
                          }}
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            </TabsContent>

            <TabsContent value="metadata" className="p-6 space-y-6">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                {/* Author */}
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="author" className="text-gray-300">Author</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="author"
                    value={formData.author_name}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, author_name: e.target.value })}
                    className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                    placeholder="Author name"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Status */}
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label className="text-gray-300">Status</Label>
                  <Select
                    value={formData.status}
                    onValueChange={(value: 'draft' | 'published' | 'archived') => 
                      setFormData({ ...formData, status: value })
                    }
                  >
                    <SelectTrigger className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent className="bg-gray-900 border-gray-700">
                      <SelectItem value="draft">Draft</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="published">Published</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="archived">Archived</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                {/* Categories */}
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="categories" className="text-gray-300">Categories</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="categories"
                    value={formData.categories}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, categories: e.target.value })}
                    className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                    placeholder="Comma-separated categories"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Tags */}
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <Label htmlFor="tags" className="text-gray-300">Tags</Label>
                  <Input
                    id="tags"
                    value={formData.tags}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, tags: e.target.value })}
                    className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                    placeholder="Comma-separated tags"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Active Status */}
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 pt-4">
                <Switch
                  id="is_active"
                  checked={formData.is_active}
                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => setFormData({ ...formData, is_active: checked })}
                />
                <Label htmlFor="is_active" className="text-gray-300">Active (visible on site)</Label>
              </div>
            </TabsContent>

            <TabsContent value="seo" className="p-6 space-y-6">
              <Card className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700">
                <CardContent className="p-6 space-y-6">
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <Label htmlFor="seo_title" className="text-gray-300">SEO Title</Label>
                    <Input
                      id="seo_title"
                      value={formData.seo_title}
                      onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, seo_title: e.target.value })}
                      className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                      placeholder="SEO optimized title"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <Label htmlFor="seo_description" className="text-gray-300">SEO Description</Label>
                    <Textarea
                      id="seo_description"
                      value={formData.seo_description}
                      onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, seo_description: e.target.value })}
                      className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
                      placeholder="SEO meta description"
                    />
                  </div>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            </TabsContent>

            {/* Action Buttons */}
            <div className="sticky bottom-0 bg-gray-900 border-t border-gray-800 p-6 flex justify-end gap-3">
              <Button
                type="button"
                variant="outline"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="border-gray-700 text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-800"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || (formData.featured_image_url && !validateImageUrl(formData.featured_image_url))}
                className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <span className="animate-spin mr-2">⏳</span>
                    {editingPost ? 'Updating...' : 'Creating...'}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    {editingPost ? 'Update Post' : 'Create Post'}
                  </>
                )}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Tabs>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

export default BlogPostFormModal;
```

```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogDescription } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

interface BlogPostFormModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
}

const BlogPostFormModal: React.FC<BlogPostFormModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSuccess }) => {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: '',
    content: '',
    featured_image_url: '',
    excerpt: '',
    author_name: ''
  });

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .insert([{
          ...formData,
          status: 'draft',
          is_active: true,
          publish_date: new Date().toISOString(),
          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
        }]);

      if (error) throw error;

      toast.success('Blog post created successfully');
      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error('Failed to create post: ' + error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[600px] bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 text-white">
        <DialogHeader className="border-b border-gray-800 pb-4">
          <DialogTitle className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Create New Blog Post
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription className="text-gray-400">
            Fill in the blog post details below
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4 py-4">
          {/* Title */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-gray-300">Title</Label>
            <Input
              id="title"
              value={formData.title}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, title: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Enter post title"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Featured Image URL */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="featured-image-url" className="text-gray-300">Featured Image URL</Label>
            <Input
              id="featured-image-url"
              type="url"
              value={formData.featured_image_url}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, featured_image_url: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="https://example.com/image.jpg"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Content */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-300">Content</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="content"
              value={formData.content}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, content: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white min-h-[200px]"
              placeholder="Write your post content"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Excerpt */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-300">Short Description</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="excerpt"
              value={formData.excerpt}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, excerpt: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Brief description of the post"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Author */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="author" className="text-gray-300">Author</Label>
            <Input
              id="author"
              value={formData.author_name}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, author_name: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Author name"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Form Actions */}
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-gray-800">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="border-gray-700 text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-800"
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white"
            >
              {loading ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Post'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

export default BlogPostFormModal;
```

I've simplified the form to include just the essential fields:
1. Title
2. Featured Image URL
3. Content
4. Short Description (Excerpt)
5. Author

The form now:
- Is a single page (no tabs)
- Has a simpler layout
- Focuses on the core functionality
- Still maintains the dark theme styling
- Creates posts as drafts by default
- Sets them as active automatically

This should be much easier to work with while still providing all the necessary functionality for creating blog posts with featured images. The URL field is properly typed as a URL input, and the form is more straightforward to use.

```typescriptreact
interface BlogPostFormModalProps {

  onSuccess: () => void;

const BlogPostFormModal: React.FC<BlogPostFormModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSuccess }) => {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: '',
    content: '',
    featured_image: '',
    excerpt: '',
    author_name: ''
  });

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .insert([{
          ...formData,
          status: 'draft',
          is_active: true,
          publish_date: new Date().toISOString(),
          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
        }]);

      if (error) throw error;

      toast.success('Blog post created successfully');
      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error('Failed to create post: ' + error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[600px] bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 text-white">
        <DialogHeader className="border-b border-gray-800 pb-4">
          <DialogTitle className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Create New Blog Post
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription className="text-gray-400">
            Fill in the blog post details below
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4 py-4">
          {/* Title */}

              id="title"

              required

              id="featured-image"
              type="url"
              value={formData.featured_image}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, featured_image: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="https://example.com/image.jpg"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Content */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-300">Content</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="content"
              value={formData.content}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, content: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white min-h-[200px]"
              placeholder="Write your post content"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Excerpt */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-300">Short Description</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="excerpt"
              value={formData.excerpt}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, excerpt: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Brief description of the post"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Author */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="author" className="text-gray-300">Author</Label>
            <Input
              id="author"
              value={formData.author_name}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, author_name: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Author name"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Form Actions */}

            </Button>
```

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import BlogPostListItem from '@/components/admin/BlogPostListItem';
import BulkImportModal from '@/components/admin/BulkImportModal';
import BlogPostEditor from '@/components/admin/BlogPostEditor';
import { Dialog, DialogContent } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { BlogPost } from '@/types/blog';

export default function BlogPostsPage() {
  const [posts, setPosts] = React.useState<BlogPost[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);
  const [showImportModal, setShowImportModal] = React.useState(false);
  const [showCreateModal, setShowCreateModal] = React.useState(false);

  const fetchPosts = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .select('*')
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

      if (error) throw error;
      setPosts(data || []);
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Error fetching posts:', error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  React.useEffect(() => {
    fetchPosts();
  }, []);

  const handleSavePost = async (postData: Partial<BlogPost>) => {
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .insert([{
          ...postData,
          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
        }]);

      if (error) throw error;

      toast.success('Blog post created successfully');
      setShowCreateModal(false);
      fetchPosts();
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Error creating post:', error);
      toast.error('Failed to create post: ' + error.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto py-8 px-4">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">Blog Posts</h1>
        <div className="flex gap-4">
          <Button
            onClick={() => setShowImportModal(true)}
            variant="outline"
            className="bg-gray-800/50 text-white hover:bg-gray-700/50 border-gray-700 hover:border-neon-cyan/30"
          >
            Bulk Import
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(true)}
            className="bg-neon-cyan text-gray-900 hover:bg-neon-cyan/90"
          >
            <Plus className="h-5 w-5 mr-2" />
            New Post
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-64">
          <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-t-2 border-b-2 border-neon-cyan"></div>
        </div>
      ) : posts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 bg-gray-800/30 rounded-lg border border-gray-700">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-2">No blog posts yet</h3>
          <p className="text-gray-400">Create your first blog post or import posts in bulk</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {posts.map((post) => (
            <BlogPostListItem
              key={post.id}
              post={post}
              onUpdate={fetchPosts}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <BulkImportModal
        isOpen={showImportModal}
        onClose={() => setShowImportModal(false)}
        onSuccess={() => {
          setShowImportModal(false);
          fetchPosts();
        }}
      />

      <Dialog open={showCreateModal} onOpenChange={setShowCreateModal}>
        <DialogContent className="max-w-4xl p-0">
          <BlogPostEditor
            post={null}
            onSave={handleSavePost}
            onCancel={() => setShowCreateModal(false)}
          />
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
```

I've made several important changes:

1. Replaced `BlogPostFormModal` with `BlogPostEditor` - this is a more complete component that includes:
   - Rich text editing for content
   - Featured image upload
   - Categories and tags
   - SEO fields
   - Post type and status selection
   - And more

2. Added proper TypeScript types for the blog posts

3. Added a proper save handler that includes all the necessary fields

4. Wrapped the editor in a Dialog component with more appropriate styling

The new editor should now work correctly and provide a much better experience for creating blog posts. When you click the "+ New Post" button, you'll see a full-featured editor with all the necessary fields, including the featured image field.

Try it now - the new editor should be working properly. Let me know if you need any adjustments or if you're still experiencing issues!

```typescriptreact
const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    
    const postData: Partial<BlogPost> = {
      title: formData.get('title') as string,
      content: formData.get('content') as string,
      excerpt: formData.get('excerpt') as string,
      featured_image: formData.get('featured_image') as string,
      post_type: formData.get('postType') as BlogPost['post_type'],
      status: formData.get('status') as BlogPost['status'],
      is_active: formData.get('isActive') === 'true',
      publish_date: formData.get('publishDate') as string,
      author_name: formData.get('authorName') as string || 'Admin',
      categories: (formData.get('categories') as string).split(',').map(c => c.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      tags: (formData.get('tags') as string).split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      seo_title: formData.get('seoTitle') as string,
      seo_description: formData.get('seoDescription') as string,
    };
```

```typescriptreact
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

interface BlogPostFormModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSuccess: () => void;
}

const BlogPostFormModal: React.FC<BlogPostFormModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onSuccess }) => {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    title: '',
    featured_image: '',
    content: '',
    excerpt: '',
    author_name: ''
  });

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .insert([{
          ...formData,
          status: 'draft',
          is_active: true,
          publish_date: new Date().toISOString(),
          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          slug: formData.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        }]);

      if (error) throw error;

      toast.success('Blog post created successfully');
      onSuccess();
      onClose();
    } catch (error: any) {
      toast.error('Failed to create post: ' + error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={isOpen} onOpenChange={onClose}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-[600px] bg-gray-900 border border-gray-700 text-white">
        <DialogHeader className="border-b border-gray-800 pb-4">
          <DialogTitle className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            Create New Blog Post
          </DialogTitle>
        </DialogHeader>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4 py-4">
          {/* Title */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-gray-300">Title</Label>
            <Input
              id="title"
              value={formData.title}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, title: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Enter post title"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Featured Image URL - Prominently displayed */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="featured_image" className="text-gray-300 flex items-center">
              Featured Image URL
              <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-neon-cyan">(Required)</span>
            </Label>
            <Input
              id="featured_image"
              type="url"
              value={formData.featured_image}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, featured_image: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white border-2 border-neon-cyan/50"
              placeholder="https://example.com/image.jpg"
              required
            />
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Enter the URL of your featured image</p>
          </div>

          {/* Content */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="content" className="text-gray-300">Content</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="content"
              value={formData.content}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, content: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white min-h-[200px]"
              placeholder="Write your post content"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Excerpt */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="excerpt" className="text-gray-300">Short Description</Label>
            <Textarea
              id="excerpt"
              value={formData.excerpt}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, excerpt: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Brief description of the post"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Author */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <Label htmlFor="author" className="text-gray-300">Author</Label>
            <Input
              id="author"
              value={formData.author_name}
              onChange={(e) => setFormData({ ...formData, author_name: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-gray-800/30 border-gray-700 text-white"
              placeholder="Author name"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Form Actions */}
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-gray-800">
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="outline"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="border-gray-700 text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-800"
            >
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="bg-neon-cyan hover:bg-neon-cyan/90 text-black font-semibold"
            >
              {loading ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Post'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
};

export default BlogPostFormModal;
```

Now let's update the page to use this simpler form:

```typescriptreact
import React from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
import BlogPostListItem from '@/components/admin/BlogPostListItem';
import BulkImportModal from '@/components/admin/BulkImportModal';
import BlogPostFormModal from '@/components/admin/BlogPostFormModal';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { BlogPost } from '@/types/blog';

export default function BlogPostsPage() {
  const [posts, setPosts] = React.useState<BlogPost[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.useState(true);
  const [showImportModal, setShowImportModal] = React.useState(false);
  const [showCreateModal, setShowCreateModal] = React.useState(false);

  const fetchPosts = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('blog_posts')
        .select('*')
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

      if (error) throw error;
      setPosts(data || []);
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error('Error fetching posts:', error.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  React.useEffect(() => {
    fetchPosts();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="container mx-auto py-8 px-4">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-white">Blog Posts</h1>
        <div className="flex gap-4">
          <Button
            onClick={() => setShowImportModal(true)}
            variant="outline"
            className="bg-gray-800/50 text-white hover:bg-gray-700/50 border-gray-700 hover:border-neon-cyan/30"
          >
            Bulk Import
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={() => setShowCreateModal(true)}
            className="bg-neon-cyan text-gray-900 hover:bg-neon-cyan/90"
          >
            <Plus className="h-5 w-5 mr-2" />
            New Post
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center items-center h-64">
          <div className="animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-t-2 border-b-2 border-neon-cyan"></div>
        </div>
      ) : posts.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 bg-gray-800/30 rounded-lg border border-gray-700">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-300 mb-2">No blog posts yet</h3>
          <p className="text-gray-400">Create your first blog post or import posts in bulk</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {posts.map((post) => (
            <BlogPostListItem
              key={post.id}
              post={post}
              onUpdate={fetchPosts}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <BulkImportModal
        isOpen={showImportModal}
        onClose={() => setShowImportModal(false)}
        onSuccess={() => {
          setShowImportModal(false);
          fetchPosts();
        }}
      />

      <BlogPostFormModal
        isOpen={showCreateModal}
        onClose={() => setShowCreateModal(false)}
        onSuccess={() => {
          setShowCreateModal(false);
          fetchPosts();
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
```

I've made two major changes:

   - Clear labeling
   - Required field indicator
   - Highlighted border
   - Helper text
   - URL type validation

2. Updated the page to use this simpler form instead of the complex editor
